### 300 subject events

- **1912.06** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Klara把空碗推回Josef Bauer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Josef Bauer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04; health 0.009999999999999898 (a good day)_

- **1913.01** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-shadow-bows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  煤气灯芯拧到最小，天花板上一件挂着的大衣被风推得晃了晃。Klara躺在摇篮里忽然笑出声，朝那片影子伸出两只手。Josef Bauer抬头看了很久，只看见衣钩和一块掉漆的墙皮。
  _effects: mood 0.02; flag +laughed_at_ceiling_shadow_

- **1914.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Helene Winter正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Klara问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Josef把Klara的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Josef说只是“替你拿着”，Klara没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.01_

- **1917.02** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  二楼窗口有人喊了一声，Klara抬头应着，脚却没动。铁环卡在Alois Berg的鞋边，Alois Berg说要再玩一圈。Klara把铁钩递过去，说明天借你。Alois Berg这才挪开脚，用鞋尖把铁环拨到Klara面前。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1917.05** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-door-was-just-coal` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, rural, agriculture
  地窖里只有煤、旧箱子和一只缺腿的凳子，Klara转了一圈，什么秘密也没找到。上楼时Josef Bauer正在切洋葱，问孩子下去做什么；Klara说找坛子，Josef Bauer便让孩子把手洗了来帮忙，案板上的洋葱辣得两人都流泪。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000002; food; flag +cellar_was_only_coal_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Emil Weiss家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Klara递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Emil Weiss端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Klara把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; skill_

- **1918.01** `kernel-child.street-play::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-play-wins` tags: childhood, neighborhood, play, injury, street, rural, agriculture, small_town
  巷子里的男孩用旧报纸裹了一个球，Klara和Emil在墙根踢。球滚进煤堆，又滚出来，黑得只剩两只脚是白的。Klara连进两个，Emil把输的弹珠推过来。回家时，Klara的膝盖破了，脸上却还挂着笑。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; health -0.010000000000000009 (scraped knee from play)_

- **1918.01** `kernel-child.household-rules::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-rules-feeds-youngest` tags: childhood, family, food, rules, everyday, rural, agriculture, small_town
  最小的弟妹在桌边哭，Klara把自己的半碗汤推过去，说在学校已经吃过。Josef Bauer看了Klara一眼，把自己的面包掰下一角，放回Klara碗里。那天晚上，Klara学会了把饥饿说成不饿。Klara把空碗推远了一点。
  _effects: relation kin with person-josef-bauer; skill; condition; flag +fed_youngest_sibling_

- **1918.04** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-sits-on-the-chair` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Elisabeth Bauer一屁股坐在那把空椅子上，说“让我也见见你的朋友”。Klara急得去拉椅子腿，两个人把盘子碰翻在地，土豆滚到灶台下。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正趴在炉边找那块土豆，嘴里还在替朋友道歉。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:26; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.03; flag +friend_defended_from_sibling; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1918.11** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-lodger-gets-it-down` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, rural, agriculture
  楼里的一个大人搬来长梯，把风筝从烟囱边挑下来。Klara在下面张着手，Anton负责扶梯子；风筝落到地上时，尾巴上多了一层黑灰。大人拍拍手走了，Klara追到楼梯口，才发现自己忘了说谢谢。
  _effects: mood 0.03; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +adult_rescued_kite_

  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1926.10** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Elisabeth Bauer写走了。Klara在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Elisabeth Bauer说没关系，把手放回Klara背后；第三支曲子响起时，Anton在门口冲Klara比了个手势，Klara假装没看见。

  Klara的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Anton骂了一句，Mitzi却把围巾解下来递给Klara；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。

  Klara在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Helene Moser 2三步远的地方拐进面包店。Klara买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 66 -> 72; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 3; mood 0.009999999999999995_

  Klara回到学校，发现同班同学已经毕业，新班级里没人认识Klara。Klara在操场上站了一下午，最后没有进教室，跟着父亲去了工地。晚上Klara把课本放进箱底，没舍得扔。工地的工头看Klara年纪小，只让Klara搬轻的。课本压在最底下。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +dropout_confirmed_

- **1927.05** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/caught-by-the-watchman` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Weiss的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Mitzi已经跑没影了，Klara的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Maria Weiss把Klara带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Klara一路上只担心Mitzi会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.04000000000000001; flag +yf18_prater_caught; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  Klara的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Alois Moser 2在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Klara在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; reputation 0.030000000000000006; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Klara天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Klara把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。
  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +day_labour_only; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/absorbed-again` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Rosa Bauer的。Klara坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Klara发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 41 -> 47; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 52 -> 54; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 8 -> 6; mood -0.03_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/called-in` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Klara听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 50 -> 58; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 55 -> 59; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 55 -> 51; mood -0.03_

- **1931.01** `major-task.relocation-klosterneuburg/moved-with-promise` tags: task, task:migration, migration, move, klosterneuburg, household, unemployment
  维也纳的房东把阁楼租金又涨了一成，Klara才下决心走。Klara在户籍办事处排了两个上午，把迁出证明和劳工证办齐。Friedrich Hofer在表格上盖了章，提醒到克洛斯特新堡后三天内去镇公所报到。月底，一家人把锅、被子和工具捆上手推车推到北站，坐火车到克洛斯特新堡，再借一辆手推车把东西推上坡，搬进姑妈家的阁楼；姑妈说葡萄园正缺短工，天亮先去问，不一定留下。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna workshop for Klosterneuburg); flag +vineyard_work_promised; cash -18 (train tickets, cart and attic rent（其中16克朗记入债务）); role; knowledge “迁出维也纳后要到镇公所登记，劳工证要随身带。”_

- **1931.04** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/widow-corrects` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  揭幕到一半，穿黑纱的Anna Bauer 2从第二排站起来，说那根火柴是她丈夫一八九三年在布尔诺登记的，专利号还刻在家里一只锡盒上。Maria Kral请她进办公室核对文件，Klara被留在展厅里，面对一队已经买票的小学生。第二天，铭牌换成了“无名热心市民”，Klara的名字被刮掉，只剩一道白痕。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:276; cash -1 (退掉车马费后的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; condition; flag +plaque_scraped_clean; mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1931.04** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005055_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Klara没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004563_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Franz Lechner把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Klara留在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Klara在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-franz-lechner; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Ferdinand Berg的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1932.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-key-and-a-title` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, rural
  办事处后门的Maria Kral把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Maria Kral没有再出现。
  _effects: organization_found 夜校同学会 (other); position 夜校同学会会长 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:005054; state_add curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:275; reputation 0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (聘书与印花的费用（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +curse_bargain_made_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 46 -> 50; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 15 -> 18; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 53 -> 51; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Klara的名字从海报上撤下；Klara在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Klara是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 5 -> 0; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 20 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 0; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.03_

- **1932.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement::civic-refugee-rural/civic-refugee-rural-barn` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, rural, agriculture, small_town
  村里人不愿意让难民住进空屋，说“谁知道他们从哪来”。她去找Emil Lechner，对方让她先担保；她签了字，Emil Weiss才被允许住进谷仓旁边的小间。谷仓的小间没有窗，她用旧麻袋堵住门缝，夜里仍能听见风。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +refugee_barn_placement; cash -1 (担保登记费); knowledge “担保难民住进谷仓旁的小间要村长登记，村里人仍会盯着出入的人”; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anna Weiss嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Anna Weiss家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Anna Weiss那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

- **1932.05** `ad20-wedding::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-band-plays-twice` tags: wedding, family, celebration, everyday, public, gossip, rural, agriculture
  乐队把同一支曲子奏了两遍，第二遍时Anna Stein 2家的老父亲已经睡着了。Klara被拉去跳舞，踩了舞伴两次脚；第三次时，两个人干脆不跳了，站在桌边看别人转圈。Klara笑得直不起腰，忘了自己本来不想来。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; cash -1 (an extra coin for the band（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +wedding_band_twice_

- **1932.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Anna Stein 2起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1933.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Ferdinand Berg没有答应，只说现在不合适。Klara把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005053_

- **1933.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/nobody-came` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, rural, agriculture
  第一次会议只来了三个人，其中一个是房东，来问房租。Klara把章程念完，没有人反对，也没有人加入。办公室的钥匙还在，但那间屋子比街上更安静。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +faction_failed_to_form_

- **1933.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999997_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005596_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Klara学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。Rosa Bauer嫌Klara变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Klara收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 55 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 4 -> 8; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 53 -> 49; mood 0.03_

  那笔私下的安排又过了一年。没有人提起，但Klara在街上遇见对方时，两个人都多停了一拍。Klara知道，沉默本身也是利息。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 47 -> 51; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 63 -> 61; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.exit 20 -> 22; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.apply-scholarship/p10-edu-work-tasks.apply-scholarship.granted` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, scholarship
  楼梯口的队伍里有穿工装的男人，也有陪女儿来的母亲。轮到他时，官员先翻推荐信，再对工资单上的数字皱眉，最后还是拿起蓝笔。他听到身后有人低声数还剩几个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (誊写申请与买信封的花费（其中1克朗记入债务）); education secondary; mood 0.1; flag +scholarship_holder_

- **1933.05** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, rural, agriculture
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Luise Winter把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Elisabeth Moser笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Klara把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1933.09** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/leave-the-table` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, rural
  Klara把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Emil Weiss耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Klara走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Klara没有停。第二天上工，Klara的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (一杯咖啡); flag +df18_refused_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000004; knowledge “我拒绝了牌桌上的钱，但手指还记得那种感觉。”_

- **1933.10** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/small-part` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, rural
  Elisabeth Stein只给了Klara三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Klara把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Klara，但乐队指挥记住了Klara每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:346; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); mood -0.020000000000000004; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:347; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:345; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  他拿不出一季度的租金，退回到城北一条巷子，改租一间和运煤工合住的屋子，两张床之间拉一根绳子挂衣服。押金四克朗照付，剩下的钱够买一星期的面包和煤球，可孩子转学的手续还压在旧房东那里。
  _effects: cash -6 (合住屋押金四克朗与搬运用度（其中2克朗记入债务）); residence; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1934.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Elisabeth Moser拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1934.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/split-per-head` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara把旧练习本翻到空白一页，写下母亲、自己和Emil的名字。十四克朗的药费单，Klara和Emil各摊五克朗，余下四克朗从母亲的养老金里扣；Klara把五克朗硬币一枚枚摞在桌沿，Emil盯着那一栏看了半天，也从围裙口袋里摸出钱来。
  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  Hermann Winter用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Klara把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Josef倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.02999999999999997; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood -0.03_

  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; flag +parish_registered_

  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:431; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Anton Lechner这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 29; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 44 -> 49; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 46 -> 50; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 14 -> 17; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 60 -> 58; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Klara的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:431; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:stage-sovereign/performance-run/lost-the-crowd` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority
  新排的戏没有坐满。Klara在第二幕听见后排有人笑，不是笑台词，是笑那顶歪掉的王冠。散场后，经理说下一轮要换一个更年轻的国王。
  _effects: state_parameter stage-sovereign.publicBelief 20 -> 12; state_parameter stage-sovereign.scandal 0 -> 6; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1938.01** `r13-work.collect-debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/wrote-it-off` tags: commerce, debt, collection, neighbor, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara在门口站了一会儿，看见Anna Stein 3家的孩子在楼梯上啃面包皮。欠条没有拿出来，Klara只说下个月再说。回家的路上，Helene Moser问这笔账还记不记，Klara说记着，但不催了；账本上那一行被折了一个角。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +work13_wrote_off_a_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; knowledge “the debtor’s household has no cash to seize”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1938.04** `r2-law.smuggling-cigarettes::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/delivery-completed` tags: crime, smuggling, customs, risk, stations, vienna, urban, crisis
  Friedrich Huber把棺材从货车上抬下来，说里面是“给乡下亲戚的遗体”，Klara在站台边接应。巡夜的手电扫过来时，Friedrich Huber故意把棺材盖滑开一条缝，露出白布；Hermann Weiss 2捂着鼻子退开，挥手放行。出了站，两人在煤堆后面拆出二十条卷烟。
  _effects: cash 18 (棺材夹层里的卷烟); condition; flag +smuggled_cigarettes_in_coffin; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; knowledge “the station guards will not inspect a coffin closely”_

- **1938.12** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara和Lena一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Klara去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Klara先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1938.12** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-horses` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  Klara把灵车和两匹马卖给了城西的殡仪馆，拿到的钱正好抵掉欠账，只剩下一盒黑手套和半瓶擦铜油。Hermann Weiss 2说这笔遗产算是结清了；Klara却总觉得，自己像替一个陌生人做了一场没有尸体的买卖。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉灵车和马匹的余款); reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; condition; flag +sold_inherited_hearse; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.01** `ad20-child-teacher::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-says-it-first` tags: family, child, school, teacher, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Lechner先开口，说老师问家里是不是有客人。Klara问怎么答的；Elisabeth Lechner说“我说有，但客人睡在厨房”。Klara笑了一下，又觉得不该笑。那天晚上，Elisabeth Lechner把厨房的门帘换成了新的。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 49 -> 51; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +child_spoke_to_teacher_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Elisabeth Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 35; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009146_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:stage-sovereign` tags: stage-sovereign, performance, theatre, pretend, status, irony, power, no-real-authority
  Klara的「舞台上的君主」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:508; flag -stage_sovereign_

- **1939.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara去了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，坐在后排听完了关于环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1931 as participant; knowledge “我参加了环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/chalk-cross-and-rent-hike` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Huber在Klara的门框上画了一个白叉，说这一层要“清理”，外国人的房租从下月起加两克朗。Klara问租约上没写这一条，Luise Huber说租约是旧政府的，现在归他解释。Gustav Bauer 2在隔壁把门开了一条缝，又轻轻关上。
  _effects: cash -4 (门上的粉笔叉（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +marked_with_chalk_cross; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the landlord marks foreign tenants with a chalk cross”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1939.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Klara把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1939.08** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/move-the-crates` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  梦里，Klara看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Klara把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Gustav Stein说Klara运气好，Klara没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/appointed-ally` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Klara把Anna Weiss的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Josef说这个岗位没有编制，Klara说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Anna Weiss带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:005054; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009642_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 35 -> 43; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 51 -> 48; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.04** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  户籍官Josef为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Klara盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Klara收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:620; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.04** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Klara刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Klara站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 1 -> 2; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 1 -> 2; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010251_

- **1940.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Hermann Weiss 2点了牛奶咖啡，Klara要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anton Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 37 -> 40; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010370_

- **1940.10** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/took-over-a-club` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  一个快要散掉的政治俱乐部让Klara接手。会员只剩十一个人，欠着两个月房租，但那间办公室的门牌还挂着。Klara在第一次会上没有讲理想，只把账本翻开。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; organization_found 接手后的俱乐部 (party); position 临时主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:010254_

- **1941.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009946_

- **1941.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sign-the-receipt` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  两名工人把Anton家的钢琴抬上卡车，琴腿在门框上磕掉一块漆。Josef把一张纸塞给Klara，说只要签“屋内无人认领”，就不必再搬第二趟。Klara签了名，看见Anton站在对面窗帘后，手一直按在玻璃上。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:619; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “签字比争辩更快，也比争辩更难忘。”; flag +signed_abandoned_piano_receipt; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Klara学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Klara的材料齐全。年底，Klara从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 25 -> 29; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 32 -> 27; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Klara在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Klara去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为普拉特动物园夜班队的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1941.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Katharina Berg 2从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Klara把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Klara把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  柜台上摊着两张工资袋，一张油墨新，一张已经磨毛了边。Klara把两袋并排放，掌柜伸出食指把旧的拨到一边，说“你管好自己的”。师兄在窗口看街对面修鞋摊，一直没回头。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001707:child; skill_

  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:689; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; flag +office_under_siege_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 46 -> 54; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 48 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Anton Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anton Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 40 -> 48; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 46 -> 43; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:fa17-starving-artist/rent-and-pages/a-year-of-work` tags: fa17-starving-artist, art, craft, poverty, recognition, work, pride, ambition
  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 54 -> 58; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 20 -> 23; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 56 -> 54; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Klara又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 85 -> 87_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Klara学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Klara的材料齐全。年底，Klara从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 29 -> 33; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 39 -> 34; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/posters-pasted` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Klara，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Klara沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

- **1942.01** `r2-law.citizenship-papers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/language-test-failed` tags: law, citizenship, papers, exam, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Lechner让Klara用官方语言读一段公民义务，Klara读到“纳税”时卡住了。Emil Lechner说可以补考，但要交两克朗，还要一张学校证明。Mitzi Lechner愿意写证明，可学校说Klara只上过夜校，没有正式学籍；Emil Lechner把材料推回来，说下次再来。
  _effects: cash -2 (入籍宣誓前的口试); condition; flag +failed_citizenship_language_test; knowledge “the language test requires a formal school certificate”_

- **1942.02** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Klara端着一只旧锅去敲Emil Weiss的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Emil Weiss让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Klara连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.04** `ad20-family-dinner::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-fourth-bowl` tags: family, dinner, everyday, obligation, public, grief, vienna, urban
  Anna Moser 4来得比约定早，手里提着一小袋栗子。Klara多摆了一副碗筷，又把汤里的肉往那只碗里拨了两块。饭吃到一半，Anna Moser 4忽然说“你们家吃饭真安静”，Klara才发现自己一直在听楼上的脚步。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; flag +family_dinner_guest; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

- **1943.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/nothing-moved` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Klara在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Josef把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.010000000000000009; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”_

- **1943.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Klara在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。

- **1943.06** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-account` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Otto Lechner 2把几张钞票压在咖啡杯底下，说只要一段能上版的细节。Klara讲了钢琴、门洞和那件湿外套；对方记得很快，临走前问能不能用真名。Klara说用化名，记者笑了一下，说化名也得像真的。
  _effects: cash 4 (sold a witness account to a reporter); state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 2; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 45 -> 60; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 89 -> 92; reputation 0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_witness_account; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  Klara把通知念给Gustav Lechner听，Gustav Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1944.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008468_

  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Rosa Bauer把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Klara去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.04999999999999999 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Anna Bauer在走廊里凑够了票数，把Klara负责的那间办公室转给了一个更听话的人。会议记录写得很干净：因工作需要。Klara回到办公室时，门牌已经换了，钥匙还能插进去，只是转不动。

  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Mitzi Stein在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Klara把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.020000000000000018 (牙病拖延未治); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  表上已经写了六个名字，Helene Stein 2问还有没有。Klara想起厨房里那张折叠床，说没有。Helene Stein 2把笔尖停在纸上，说“想清楚，漏报要罚”。Klara还是说没有；出门时，听见自己的声音在走廊里回了一下。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 49 -> 53; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 75 -> 79; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 21 -> 18; flag +census_hidden_name; knowledge “漏报要罚，但厨房里那张折叠床没有写进表。”_

- **1946.09** `r3-mig.family-reunification::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sibling-refused-liability` tags: migration, family, sponsorship, papers, debt, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Lechner念到“被担保人失业或生病，由担保人承担费用”时，Emil的笔停在纸上。Emil说自己有两个孩子，房租已经欠了两个月。Klara说不怪你。回家的电车上，两个人一路没有说话，Emil提前一站下了车。

- **1948.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Klara听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Rosa Bauer 2扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999994 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

- **1948.06** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Klara把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Berta Hofer站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Klara手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002136:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Klara把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Maria Hofer递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻)_

  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work_

  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂)_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; health 0.020000000000000018 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

  教堂后院的铁锅冒着白汽，排队的人把各种罐子捧在胸前。Emil Lechner穿着灰围裙一勺一勺地分，轮到Klara时锅底已经露出来，勺子刮出响声，还是刮了半勺倒进罐里。
  _effects: food; health 0.030000000000000027 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1953.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015750_

- **1953.01** `r15-wish.power-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/protected-ally` tags: politics, power, wish, office, betrayal, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anna Weiss被指控挪用公款。Klara用自己刚拿到的签字权压下了调查，条件是Anna Weiss从此在委员会里站自己这一边。Klara知道这不是干净的交易，也知道自己已经离不开它。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:1157_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016974_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018411_

- **1953.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-price-is-a-name` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  Maria Weiss只要Klara在一张空白委任状上写下真名，说剩下的由办事处去办。一个月过去，职位没有来，Klara的名字却出现在一份领款单上，金额和签名都不属于Klara。Maria Weiss调走了，窗口换了人，没有人愿意查那张纸是从哪一叠里抽出来的。
  _effects: cash -2 (打点办事处的钱（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +name_used_without_office; reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Klara把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Klara忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 65 -> 73; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 10 -> 8_

- **1953.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Klara找到Emil，把接手后的俱乐部的章程和会费说清楚；对方留下地址，答应下次再来。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “我通过Klara加入了接手后的俱乐部。”_

- **1953.07** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sign-here` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Luise Stein把借据铺在桌上，先让Klara看利息，又让Klara看还款日期。Klara用指头一行行划过去，在最后一行停住：若逾期，担保人承担。Luise Stein说只是走个形式；Klara还是把担保人的名字问清楚，才签了字。
  _effects: cash 6 (a loan was signed with a private creditor); state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:1212; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002189:child; flag +loan_signed; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “借据上写着若逾期，担保人承担。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1953.08** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  旧锅底还沾着昨天的面糊，Klara拿它盛炭时有点不好意思。Anna 2没说什么，只把火钳递过来，让她自己夹。炭在锅里红得发暗，一路端回屋没灭。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006169:child; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Luise Lechner一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Luise Lechner的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003123:child; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Klara把口袋里的面包屑撒在窗台上。乌鸦飞下来啄食，Luise Moser 2在窗内看见，笑了一声，说这案子要是也有这么好喂就好了。几天后，Luise Moser 2把一份旧记录的抄件塞给Klara，说别问是谁给的。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 10; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; knowledge “法院里的官员给了我一份旧记录抄件。”; flag +df18_clerk_gave_copy; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Klara把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Klara忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。

  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Klara听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 45 -> 53; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 60 -> 64; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 50 -> 46_

- **1954.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara去了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，坐在后排听完了关于环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31-evt:world-vienna-1912:012866-1947 as participant; knowledge “我参加了环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.06** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1954.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Klara只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1954.12** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1954.12** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  圣文生会的修女在登记簿上写下Klara的门牌号，递过面包券和一小袋土豆。Mitzi Lechner在旁边按了指印作保，说这户男人在工厂有活。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Luise Lechner嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Luise Lechner家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Luise Lechner那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003123:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

  名单在委员会卡住了。Marie Huber的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Klara没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Marie Huber来问结果，Klara说再等一年；Marie Huber没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。

- **1955.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1955.05** `kernel-social.shared-stove::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::shared-stove-scarcity/shared-stove.coal-taken` tags: social, neighbor, everyday, laundry, cooperation, conflict, vienna, urban
  Otto Lechner 2用了Klara的煤，却没有添回去。Klara把空煤箱提到她门口，Otto Lechner 2说只借了一铲，等煤车来就还；煤车晚了两天，两家在走廊里碰见都绕着走。煤箱底下还留着上一场雪的湿痕。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; cash -2 (replacing the coal taken from the box); role; flag +coal_taken_

- **1955.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

  新法规定老房子租金不得超过战前水平的两成。Klara拿着租约去登记，Rosa算完说Maria Bauer 2多收了三克朗。Maria Bauer 2在窗口前争辩，说修屋顶的钱没人管；Rosa把登记章盖下去，说修屋顶另填一张表。

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; health -0.040000000000000036 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-mitzi-lechner_

### 307 subject events

- **1912.06** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Mitzi忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Alois Lechner看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +first_word_door_

- **1913.02** `vienna-infant-household.cradle-fall/floor-thud` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Mitzi从摇床缺口翻出来，后脑磕在木地板上，闷响一声。Alois Lechner正在灶边热奶，扔下锅跑过来，孩子哭得一口气堵在喉咙里。当晚Alois Lechner拆了旧木箱，钉了一块挡板。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (后脑磕在地板上); cash -0.5 (买了半把铁钉)_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Mitzi问还回不回来，Alois Lechner把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Emil Weiss的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Mitzi坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Emil Weiss把桶一放，夹起Mitzi进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Alois Lechner赶来时，Mitzi正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  Friedrich Hofer把Mitzi的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Alois Lechner赶来时，Friedrich Hofer说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Alois Lechner的手一直攥着Mitzi的袖子。

  下一站，Mitzi追下车，把那只手套递还给Josef。Josef说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Mitzi回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。

  Mitzi把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Mitzi把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Alois Lechner醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Mitzi说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.02999999999999997; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +yf18_unpacked_

  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Mitzi把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Alois Lechner的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:122; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Mitzi把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Mitzi没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 60 -> 58_

- **1926.08** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1926.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1926.09** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在第二支曲子前把Josef请走，说Mitzi答应过要替她看管外套。Mitzi抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Mitzi把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 28 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1926.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  Mitzi把袖口卷到肘上，用麻绳扎紧，替圣马克斯殡葬合作社把一叠纸送进压印机。前两百张很顺，第二百零一张时，纸角卡住，Mitzi伸手去拨，机器没有停；等Alois Moser 2拉开Mitzi，食指的指甲已经翻了起来。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (a hand caught in an unguarded press); condition; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 22; cash -2 (a doctor and a bandage); flag +yf18_press_injury; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Mitzi背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Mitzi回家时Gustav Lechner还躺着，Mitzi把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Gustav Lechner自己坐起来端碗，Mitzi才觉得肩膀没那么沉。

  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

  Alois Bauer把老花镜往鼻梁上推了推，让Mitzi用食指在字盘空位上比画。咖啡馆伙计来续水时，老人把字母表折起来，说下星期带旧字样本来看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +typesetting_mentor; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1929.01** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.rival-at-table/rival-ask-around` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Mitzi没有当场争，只在回去的路上向熟人打听那位助理在区公所拿多少薪水、住哪条街。听说对方按月领钱、家里还有老宅，Mitzi算了算自己的工钱，第二天到铺子里问师傅能不能多排几个晚班；比较之下，工钱薄得像一张纸，但也学会了先把账问清再开口。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1929.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Ferdinand Huber点了牛奶咖啡，Mitzi要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Ferdinand Huber没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Mitzi回头看了一眼，Ferdinand Huber正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

  Franz Winter 2没有接话，先从抽屉里撕下一角纸。Mitzi把名字、住址和手艺一样样说完，工头边写边应，写完把纸压回抽屉，说月底来问。

- **1930.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Mitzi把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999994 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Mitzi把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Mitzi读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Mitzi在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Mitzi拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Rosa找到Mitzi时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Mitzi当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.029999999999999995; relation colleague with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1931.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Alois Lechner放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Elisabeth Bauer在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Mitzi，Alois Lechner把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; food_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Mitzi不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Mitzi拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Mitzi的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi在Magistrat, Bezirksstelle Leopoldstadt靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Friedrich Kral 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1932.04** `ad20-lucky-coincidence::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government/the-wrong-coin` tags: luck, coincidence, everyday, money, superstition, debt, reform, medical
  Mitzi把硬币放进嘴里咬了一下，发现是铁的，上面印着一家早已关门的咖啡馆。Mitzi没有扔，把它放在窗台上压纸。后来每次风大，那枚假币就在窗台上转半圈，像一个不肯走的小客人。
  _effects: cash -1 (a fake coin was not accepted at the shop); mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +token_not_coin; knowledge “硬币上印着一家早已关门的咖啡馆。”_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  报纸的社会版把Mitzi家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, migration, unemployment
  Mitzi听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

- **1937.01** `p9-work-career.night-course/p9-work-career.night-course.dropout` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, night_school, bookkeeping
  他第三次推门时，Mitzi Lechner正讲到结账，抬眼看了他一下，没说什么，只朝后排的空位点了点头。Mitzi坐下，笔记本还停在上次的页码。
  _effects: cash -6 (学费不退，等于白付); mood -0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1937.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Alois Lechner放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Rosa Moser在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Mitzi，Alois Lechner把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000332:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; food_

- **1937.10** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1937.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Katharina Bauer 3说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Katharina Bauer 3陪着Mitzi去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1937.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006169_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Mitzi在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Hermann Weiss 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Mitzi带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Mitzi咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Mitzi手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Mitzi Lechner等在门口，问值不值，Mitzi只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill_

- **1938.01** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-cart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  车夫把价钱咬得很死，Mitzi还到五克朗又添了二十赫勒，才说定跑一趟。Berta Weiss扶着床架，车过路口时煤箱歪了一下，细灰撒在路面上。到了地方，东西卸在门厅，天已经大亮。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1938.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1938.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1938.06** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.lost-dog/lost-dog.chase-two-streets` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, animal, neighbor
  狗跑得不算快，可它拐弯时总挑窄巷子钻。Mitzi跟着它穿过一条挂满洗好的床单的过道，床单扫过她的额头。等她站定，狗已经不见影了，脚上的旧鞋咧开了嘴，露出沾了泥的袜尖。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销)_

- **1939.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-morphine-trade` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Mitzi用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。
  _effects: cash -20 (boots and cash traded for two morphine ampoules); health 0.019999999999999997 (pain relief from a black-market ampoule); role; flag +traded_for_morphine; knowledge “军需处先领走奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林；旧靴子可以换两支吗啡。”_

- **1939.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi把通知念给Luise Kral听，Luise Kral摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Mitzi去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.sister` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  姐姐把孩子裹在披肩里抱上电车，说城外有院子，一个月就送回来。Mitzi站在站台上看车走远。一个月后孩子会叫人了，可看见Mitzi就往姐姐身后躲。Mitzi把孩子抱进屋，他哭了半宿。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation kin with person-franz-lechner_

- **1940.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工资袋上的油墨还没干透，Mitzi的手指染了一点黑。母亲把硬币倒在厨房桌上，一枚枚排开，排到第六枚时停住。灶上的水壶响了，她拿起来又放下，没继续问。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1941.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-to-sign` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把笔推回去，说钢琴的主人就住在隔壁。Emil Lechner没有争，只让工人把琴盖也搬走，又记下Mitzi的门牌号。卡车开走时，Anna Stein 3家的窗子开了又关；Mitzi站在空出来的门洞前，听见自己的呼吸声。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:622; reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +refused_piano_receipt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1941.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.rain-turn-back` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  走到半路雨点砸下来，山道立刻变得溜滑。Mitzi和Lena躲进路边的酒棚檐下，商量了几句，决定改乘电车回家，车票六十赫勒。Mitzi把带去的面包掰开分给同车躲雨的人，自己站在车尾，湿透的裤脚贴着腿，一路打着哆嗦；远足没走完，倒记住了一车陌生人的谢意。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (改乘电车回家的车票); food; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1941.04** `r3-mig.residency-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/paid-for-doorplate` tags: migration, registration, housing, papers, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi交了五克朗登记费和一克朗“门牌费”，Josef从抽屉里取出一块蓝底白字的门牌，说钉在门口，警察看见就知道这户登记过。Anna Bauer接过门牌，翻过来看了看背面，说钉子要自己买。
  _effects: cash -6 (门牌号与登记簿); condition; flag +paid_registration_and_doorplate_fee; relation colleague with person-anna-bauer; knowledge “the blue doorplate signals a registered address”_

- **1941.09** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010757_

  Mitzi伸手关掉收音机，说电池要留给夜里的急救。Maria 2不同意，两人在黑暗里摸到同一只旋钮。最后谁也没有再开；楼道里只剩邻居的怀表在走，每一下都像在催人下楼。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:691; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000920:child; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +silenced_radio_during_blackout_

  Mitzi把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Emil Moser低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1942.01** `fm17-consolation.kitchen::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-empty-chair` tags: faith, consolation, grief, family, neighbor, care, quiet, vienna
  Mitzi没有开门，只说今天不想见人。Josef把汤留在门口，压了一张纸条：不用回话。天黑以后，Mitzi把汤端进来，坐在那张空椅子对面吃完；碗底有一层薄薄的扁豆，她没有倒掉。
  _effects: food; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +fm17_consolation_at_the_door_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Mitzi听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 90 -> 82_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Mitzi在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.keep-family-secret::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::secret-crisis/police-search` tags: task, family, secret, reputation, sibling, fear, vienna, urban
  两个便衣跟着门房上楼，说要查一份“反动传单”。Mitzi把柜底的纸塞进Friedrich Kral 2送来的面粉袋，又抓了一把面盖住。Otto Lechner站在门口拖时间，说家里只有老人和孩子；便衣翻到米缸时，Mitzi的膝盖一直在抖。
  _effects: condition; flag +hid_paper_during_police_search; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child; knowledge “the police were looking for a printed leaflet, not the famil”_

- **1942.02** `ld18-wedding.private-rite::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-landlady-applauds` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, secret, ritual, commitment, secret_intimacy
  仪式进行到一半，Franz Berg推门进来，以为她们在试婚纱。她坚持要喝一杯，还说明天可以借出院子里的葡萄架再办一次。Mitzi和Berta Bauer 2面面相觑，最后真的在葡萄架下又交换了一次戒指，这次有六个人围观。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:743; state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:744; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +impossible_attachment_active; flag +public_desire_active_

  Mitzi只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Katharina Kral手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Katharina Kral把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Mitzi记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。

  在柜台前把话问出口，对方愣了一下，说星期天下午可以。两张楼座票一共两克朗二十赫勒，约在剧院侧门前见。对方把那叠乐谱往旁边推了推，说散场后可以沿环城道走一段。
  _effects: cash -2.2 (两张星期天轻歌剧楼座票); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 30 -> 33; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Franz Berg低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; reputation 0.039999999999999994; skill_

- **1943.10** `ld18-child.discovery::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-tells-the-teacher` tags: family, child, secret, queer, discovery, raising_child, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Grete Lechner在学校说，家里有两个妈妈。老师把Mitzi叫去，问是不是家庭情况“复杂”；第二天，Maria Weiss来接孩子时，门口多了几个家长。Grete Lechner牵着Maria Weiss的手，走得很慢，像在等Mitzi跟上来。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:795; reputation -0.05000000000000002; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007702:child; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 31 -> 41; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag -public_desire_active; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1943.12** `ad20-landlord-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repairs-promised` tags: housing, rent, landlord, everyday, repair, public, vienna, urban
  Marie Huber带着木尺来了，先敲窗框，又敲暖气片。Mitzi把去年冬天裂开的缝指给他看；Marie Huber说可以修，但材料钱要从下个月房租里扣。Mitzi算了算，还是点了头。窗外的雨顺着玻璃往下走，屋里第一次没有风。
  _effects: cash -1 (materials for the window repair（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; flag +repair_promised; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `ld18-admirer.gift::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-gift-is-returned` tags: admirer, gift, public_desire, jealousy, danger, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban
  Mitzi在剧院后门把钥匙还给Maria Weiss，说自己不接没有名字的礼物。Maria Weiss把钥匙收回掌心，说名字不重要，重要的是她肯不肯来。Mitzi说肯不肯都不去；第二天，后台的花篮少了一只。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 10 -> 20; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 27 -> 22; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一个亲戚来借住，顺手问起那只箱子。Mitzi说钥匙不在手边；对方走后，Mitzi把箱子从柜顶搬到床下，又觉得床下太潮。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.access 20 -> 16; state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 28 -> 33; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 41 -> 47; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 32 -> 29; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 41; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 30 -> 32; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1946.01** `ad20-child-teacher::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-has-a-question` tags: family, child, school, teacher, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Lechner问Mitzi，Anna 2最近为什么总在课上打瞌睡。Mitzi说家里晚上在赶一批活；Mitzi Lechner点点头，说“别让孩子熬太晚”。Mitzi应了一声，心里却在算那批活还能不能按时交。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 41 -> 43; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +teacher_asked_homework_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:em18-family-archive/archive-year/damp-and-mice` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  雨季过后，箱底的文件粘在一起，边角被老鼠咬掉。Mitzi用熨斗隔着布把纸烘干，字迹还是淡了一半；一张能证明工龄的收据，从此只剩下半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 18 -> 14; state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 41 -> 43; cash -1 (new paper and a visit to the notary（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 78 -> 80_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Mitzi把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Mitzi这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 57 -> 63; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 30 -> 25; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 58 -> 62; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 44 -> 50; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 29 -> 26; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 37 -> 33; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 42 -> 44; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/someone-vouches` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  一个不算亲近的人替Mitzi说了句话：不是保证Mitzi已经变好，只说那天Mitzi确实在场、确实没有躲。话传回来时，Mitzi正在补一只旧口袋，针脚第一次没有歪。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 63 -> 70; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 62 -> 67; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014042_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Stein 2把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Mitzi去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Anna Lechner 2给Mitzi一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Mitzi把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1946.06** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/recognize-a-neighbor` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  银幕上是城西的街口，镜头扫过一排晾衣绳。Mitzi认出绳下站着Anna Weiss，也认出旁边那个被两名便衣夹住的人。散场灯亮时，Anna Weiss还在笑，Mitzi却把围巾往上拉了拉。
  _effects: knowledge “新闻片里那个被便衣夹住的人，我认识。”; flag +recognized_neighbor_on_newsreel; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1946.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi正要把空床许给Elisabeth Huber 2，Anna Stein 3却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Mitzi回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Elisabeth Huber 2，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

- **1946.10** `sm18-object.heirloom-missing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/reported-before-the-will-is-read` tags: object, loss, inheritance, family, suspicion, everyday, vienna, urban
  律师刚走，Franz Lechner就在祖母的针线盒里发现少了一只银胸针。Mitzi记得最后一次见它是在遗嘱签字那天，胸针别在祖母的灰披肩上。Franz Lechner说先别声张，免得帮佣听见；Mitzi却已经把空盒子端到了厨房门口。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:927; relation kin with person-franz-lechner; flag +heirloom_reported_missing; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1946.10** `state_reaction:em18-family-archive/inheritance-claim` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  律师念到“旧信件与照片归……”时停了一下，因为清单上没有写清楚。Mitzi回家后把箱子重新翻了一遍，发现少了一张已经泛黄的收据；没有人承认拿过。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 43 -> 51; state_parameter em18-family-archive.access 16 -> 11; knowledge “遗产清单上有一张已经泛黄的收据，后来不见了。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014402_

- **1947.01** `ad20-school-meeting::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/teacher-blames` tags: family, school, child, meeting, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Lechner说Anna 2这学期退步，是因为家里晚上太吵。Mitzi想说自己夜里在做计件活，话到嘴边又咽回去；Anna 2站在走廊尽头，书包带子断了一边。回家的路上，Mitzi把书包带打了个结，谁也没有提老师的话。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 50 -> 46; cash -1 (extra exercise books after the meeting（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 44 -> 47; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把Rosa 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

  半年过去，Mitzi又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 20 -> 26; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 10 -> 8; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `r14-soc.borrow-from-usurer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pawned-the-watch` tags: task, criminal_debt, debt, money, fear, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi没有借满，只拿了五克朗，把父亲的怀表留在柜台上。Hermann Weiss打开表盖看了看，说表走得不准，只能算这个价。Mitzi把多出的一克朗推回去，拿走了当票。当票上的字很小，Mitzi在门口就着煤气灯看了两遍，才认出父亲的表号。表盖里面刻着祖父的名字，Hermann Weiss没有看见。
  _effects: cash 5 (钟表铺后间的算盘); mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +pawned_watch_to_usurer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1947.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi上楼敲了Alois Berg的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Alois Berg把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Mitzi能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Rosa Berg把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Mitzi留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Mitzi在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001707:child; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Mitzi被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Mitzi低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 56 -> 66; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015070_

  头发刚擦到一半，Alois Berg来敲门收煤气钱。Mitzi把Maria Weiss推进壁橱，自己披上外套去开门；床上的湿毛巾还没来得及收。Alois Berg看了一眼，说下次记得把水倒了，楼梯会滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 66 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 23 -> 28; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Mitzi和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Mitzi在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 69 -> 73; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 74 -> 71; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Mitzi忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 8 -> 16; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 26 -> 24; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `kernel-civic.petition-drive::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-petition-refused` tags: politics, petition, reform, neighbor, organization, vienna, urban, postwar
  她把签名纸送到市政厅，Emil Lechner说格式不对，要按街道分开。她回去重抄，发现有几个名字已经认不出；再去时，窗口换了一个人，说前一班没有交接。她坐在市政厅台阶上重抄，风把纸吹到花坛里。
  _effects: flag +petition_refused; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱); health -0.010000000000000002 (来回跑市政厅后的疲惫); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1948.04** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-mine-was-salted` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  抽水机运到山里才发现，矿洞的岩壁上早被人撒过金粉，岩芯也是挑过的。Franz Bauer 2在两个月前把股份转给了一家空壳公司，办公室只剩一张桌子和半瓶墨水。Helene Winter 3把调查报告放在Mitzi面前，最后一页写着“建议不予起诉”，因为找不到被告。
  _effects: cash -50 (the investment in a salted mine（其中50克朗记入债务）); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1015; condition; flag +salted_mine; knowledge “矿洞岩壁上的金粉是撒上去的，岩芯也被挑过。”; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi拿不出押金，就在市场租了一个半张桌子的摊位，卖线和纽扣。第一天只卖出三卷线，Josef的摊子却排着队。Mitzi收摊时把剩货装进木箱，决定明天早点来，占住靠门的那一头。
  _effects: cash -6 (租下街角的空铺（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +started_as_stallholder; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1949.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015443_

- **1949.01** `pc20-family.secret-papers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/confront` tags: family, secret, archive, letter, public_reversal, sibling, reputation, vienna
  Mitzi把信放在Rosa面前，没有问是不是他写的，只问当年为什么没有人说。Rosa看了很久，最后说因为说出来会让另一个人活不下去；Mitzi把信折好，第一次没有替家里做决定。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1014; relation kin with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +confronted_family_secret; knowledge “当年没有人说，是因为说出来会让另一个人活不下去。”; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/another-turn-of-the-screw` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Mitzi在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -55 -> -63; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 55 -> 63; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Mitzi把Rosa 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 57 -> 65; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 48 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi把Anna 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anna 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

  Mitzi的「往故乡汇钱的户」到期；相关安排结束。
