- **1949.01** `state_tick:em18-family-archive/archive-year/a-box-gets-opened` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  又一年过去，Mitzi从柜顶取下一只纸箱，里面的信封按年份捆着，绳子一碰就碎。Mitzi把出生证、地契和一张没寄出的明信片分开，桌上多出三小堆纸；最小的那一堆，Mitzi没有让别人看。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 6 -> 10; state_parameter em18-family-archive.secrets 37 -> 40; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “家里的纸箱里有一张没有寄出的明信片。”_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 71 -> 77; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 28 -> 25; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Mitzi把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Mitzi忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 16 -> 24; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 24 -> 22; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.confront-rumour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rumor-spread` tags: task, social, rumor, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi越说越快，Emil Weiss反而往后退，说被冤枉的人才会这么急。Alois Moser 3低头搓衣服，一句话也没接。第二天，Mitzi去市场买盐，摊主把秤盘往回一收，说今天不赊账。
  _effects: reputation -0.10000000000000002; condition; flag +rumor_grew_after_confrontation; relation rival with person-emil-weiss_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Mitzi在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Mitzi时停住。晚上，Mitzi把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 24 -> 34; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016165_

- **1949.07** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ignore-the-dream` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Mitzi醒后只当是晚饭太咸，翻个身又睡了。下午，地窖的水管爆开，煤和旧箱子泡在齐膝的水里。Alois Weiss帮着舀水，Mitzi一边舀一边想起梦里的水位，正好到第二个台阶。
  _effects: cash -4 (泡坏的煤和旧箱子（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; knowledge “我没有理会梦，地窖真的淹了。”; flag +df18_ignored_flood_dream; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.12** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/clap-with-the-crowd` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  新闻片放到新修的桥，观众跟着鼓掌。Mitzi也拍了两下手，掌心却停在半空——画面角落里那家面包店，昨天还排着另一条队。Rosa Bauer 2说下回还来，Mitzi说票价太贵。
  _effects: cash -1 (two cinema tickets); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +clapped_at_the_newsreel_

- **1950.01** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-medal-for-model-household` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  道德协会把一枚铜牌送到Mitzi和Maria Weiss的店里，说她们的铺子窗明几净、账目清楚，是模范家庭。Berta Huber 2握着她们的手，问什么时候请吃喜酒；Mitzi说等生意再好一点，Maria Weiss在旁边把抹布拧成了麻花。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; cash 2 (the prize money and the new customers); state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:1057; flag +model_household_medal; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Mitzi的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Mitzi怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 10 -> 15; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016495_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Mitzi被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Mitzi低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 77 -> 87; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 4 -> 8; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016495_

  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Mitzi说伴侣是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 87 -> 99; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 25 -> 17; reputation -0.03; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Mitzi上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Mitzi把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -71 -> -61; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 71 -> 66; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.join-choir::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/audition-passed` tags: culture, choir, music, community, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Lechner让Mitzi先唱一段熟悉的赞美诗，Mitzi的声音在空礼拜堂里撞上石墙，又落回来。Mitzi Lechner按了几个键，说低声部缺人，下周四来排练。Mitzi把歌本夹在腋下，走出门时脚步轻了。
  _effects: flag +joined_choir; role; skill; relation colleague with person-mitzi-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

- **1950.01** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/correct-it-twice` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  Mitzi在窗口要求更正，Emil Lechner把表格翻了三遍，最后开出一张新的出生证明，名字却少了一个字母。于是城里同时住着一个死人、一个活人，和一个只在档案里出生的陌生人。Mitzi拿着两张纸站在区公所门口，忽然不知道该用哪一张去领下个月的煤票。
  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:1105; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Mitzi刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Mitzi站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 27; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017235_

- **1950.08** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Anna Bauer追出来问怎么不领了，Mitzi说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Mitzi把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: food_

- **1950.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa 2这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 65 -> 68; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017317_

- **1951.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Mitzi一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Anton送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1951.01** `sm18-object.lost-property::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-object-is-in-the-drawer` tags: object, loss, office, search, bureaucracy, everyday, vienna, urban
  失物招领处在一间半地下室里，架子上按月份捆着纸包。Emil Lechner翻到第三本登记簿，把一只用麻线扎紧的小包推过来。Mitzi报出针扣上的刻字，Emil Lechner点头，收了两克朗保管费。
  _effects: cash -2 (失物招领处的保管费（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:927; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “失物一直在失物招领处的第三本登记簿下面”; flag +recovered_object_at_lost_property; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:pc20-public-reversal/file-moves/the-file-disappears` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  Mitzi按约定时间去调卷，窗口的人说编号不对，又说管库的人病了。走廊尽头的门开着，里面只剩空架子和一层灰；Mitzi把手伸进去，摸到的是一截断掉的麻绳。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 52 -> 44; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 42 -> 48; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 54 -> 59; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Mitzi，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Mitzi没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 38; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 15 -> 19; reputation -0.030000000000000013_

  这一年，Mitzi学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Mitzi的材料齐全。年底，Mitzi从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 40 -> 44; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 27 -> 22; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `state_transition:ld18-impossible-attachment/papers-become-public` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Mitzi从「无法登记的伴侣」转入另一段处境：the pressure made the attachment publicly legible
  _effects: state_transition ld18-impossible-attachment -> known-affair; reputation -0.1; flag +unregistered_attachment_exposed; flag -impossible_attachment_active; flag +known_affair_active_

- **1951.01** `r14-hist.reconstruction-housing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/repaired-the-cellar-first` tags: reconstruction_housing, housing, postwar, repair, vienna, urban, reform, scarcity
  重建贷款只够修屋顶，Mitzi决定先修地窖，因为邻居家的煤和土豆都堆在那里。Katharina Kral教Mitzi怎么把旧砖上的砂浆敲掉，再用石灰重新砌。地下室干了三天，墙上还是渗水；Mitzi在角落里放了一只铁盆，夜里听见水滴进去，像有人在数拍子。
  _effects: cash -18 (废墟上的三层砖（其中18克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +cellar_repaired; skill_

- **1951.02** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-candle-stall` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  Rosa Berg 2在门口摆了一只木箱，卖蜡烛和印着水渍的小卡片，说摸一摸就能好。Mitzi看着邻居把零钱收进围裙，没有阻止。几天后，楼道里排起了队；Mitzi从后门进出，怕被人认成那个“显灵”的女人。
  _effects: cash 2 (邻居分给subject的蜡烛钱); reputation 0.01999999999999999; knowledge “邻居把我的水渍变成了卖蜡烛的摊子。”; flag +df18_water_stain_shrine; condition; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1951.09** `ml18-luck.wrong-horse::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-horse-wins` tags: gambling, luck, money, horse_race, absurd, windfall, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把“蓝多瑙”看成了“蓝星期一”，在柜台前把五克朗押在一匹没人看好的马上。最后一道弯，那匹马从外道切进来，Anton Bauer手里的铅笔停在票面上。赔率是一赔十二；Berta Berg把赢来的钱数了两遍，Mitzi还在看自己的票，像不相信上面的名字。
  _effects: cash 60 (winnings from the wrong horse at long odds); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1141; reputation 0.03; flag +wrong_horse_won; mood 0.08999999999999997; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Mitzi和Berta Berg合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; household; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

- **1952.01** `p21-blackmail-wedding::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-ring-goes-on` tags: blackmail, wedding, marriage, known-affair, scandal, power, love, secret
  戒指还是戴上了。Berta Berg的母亲坐在第一排，手里攥着一串念珠；Anna Lechner 4没有来，但托人送来一只空信封。Mitzi在婚宴上敬了三桌酒，回到新房才发现门锁已经换了，钥匙放在门垫下面。邻居在门外放了一挂鞭。
  _effects: state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:1140; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155; cash 3 (an installment of the partner family dowry); flag +forced_wedding_completed; condition; knowledge “婚宴上少了一个人，门垫下多了一把新钥匙。”; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; flag +scandal_web_active_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Mitzi的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Mitzi怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 53 -> 61; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 19 -> 24; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017948_

  新的丑闻登出来时，旧的那一页也被重新翻到桌面上。Mitzi发现两件事共用同一个证人，而那个证人上周还来借过盐。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 45 -> 51; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 45 -> 49; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 20 -> 17_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:pc20-public-reversal/file-moves/the-file-disappears` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  Mitzi按约定时间去调卷，窗口的人说编号不对，又说管库的人病了。走廊尽头的门开着，里面只剩空架子和一层灰；Mitzi把手伸进去，摸到的是一截断掉的麻绳。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 44 -> 36; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 48 -> 54; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 59 -> 64; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Mitzi，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Mitzi没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 38 -> 46; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 24 -> 28; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Mitzi的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Mitzi收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Mitzi先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 44 -> 51; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 22 -> 32; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/forgotten` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Mitzi的名字从闲谈里慢慢退了下去。街上有了新的婚礼、新的葬礼和新的丑闻；只有裁缝还记得Mitzi改过两次同一件外套的腰身。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 30 -> 25; state_parameter known-affair.grief 8 -> 5; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/soup-kitchen-opened` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把工会活动室的长桌拼起来，用两口大锅煮扁豆汤。Katharina Kral 2赊了半袋豆子和一筐洋葱，条件是罢工结束后先还他。第一天来了三十七个人，Mitzi给每个碗里多舀半勺；汤不够时，就往锅里再添一壶水。
  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; organization_funds; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1952.06** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-damaged-deducted` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor, injury
  新住处的门框比旧屋矮，柜子抬进去时顶上的雕花蹭掉一块。Maria Lechner没当场发作，结账时才从工钱里扣了去，说修补漆得花钱。Luise Kral 2在旁边直摇头，到底没帮着说话。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (搬家磕坏柜脚被扣工钱); health -0.020000000000000004 (搬柜子时手背划伤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147_

- **1952.07** `state_reaction:p21-scandal-web/a-betrayal-reprices` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  背叛发生以后，Mitzi最先想到的不是损失，而是那个人知道多少。第二天，对方在街角点了点头，像在提醒Mitzi：现在两个人都有东西可以卖了。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 17 -> 9; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 80 -> 88; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 49 -> 54; flag +scandal_web_betrayed_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018573_

- **1952.08** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-hand` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  一连三天的高烧退下去以后，Mitzi的手腕上多了一块暗褐色的印子，边缘像五根很短的手指。诊所的医生说只是皮下色素，Mitzi Lechner却在院子里看了很久，说这形状不是随便长的。Mitzi把袖子拉下来，回家路上一直用另一只手按着那块皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (去诊所问这块印子); health -0.03 (高烧后的虚弱); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:1176; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_fever; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1952.08** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Mitzi在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Mitzi说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 25 -> 33; reputation -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018655_

- **1952.11** `ad20-landlord-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rent-raised` tags: housing, rent, landlord, everyday, repair, public, vienna, urban
  Katharina Kral看完窗子，说这栋楼的地税涨了，房租也得跟着涨。Mitzi把租约摊在桌上，指给他看还有半年；Katharina Kral说“那就从下半年开始”。他走后，Mitzi把窗缝用旧报纸塞住，报纸上正好登着新房租的广告。
  _effects: cash -2 (rent increase after the landlord’s visit); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; flag +rent_raised; knowledge “房东说地税涨了，房租从下半年开始加。”_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:known-affair/betrayal-in-public` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_reaction
  背叛被讲出来以后，Mitzi的名字又上了一轮饭桌。有人替Mitzi不平，有人只想知道细节；Mitzi把杯子洗干净，放回柜子，决定这周不去市场。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 25 -> 40; state_parameter known-affair.grief 5 -> 15_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018555_

- **1953.01** `ld18-scandal.rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-story-grows-teeth` tags: scandal, rumor, public, neighbor, desire, reputation, secret_intimacy, vienna
  有人在洗衣房说，Mitzi星期三下午进过一家旅馆，出来时头发是湿的。话传到市场，已经变成另一个版本；Maria Weiss说得最细，连房间号都编好了。Mitzi没有解释，只把星期三的工票钉在厨房墙上。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:1175; reputation -0.07999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 40 -> 50; mood -0.029999999999999916_

  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Mitzi去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 5 -> 0; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 20 -> 28_

  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 50 -> 58; reputation -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 51 -> 48; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 9 -> 13; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 54 -> 51; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  入冬后，Mitzi在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Mitzi低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 33 -> 43; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 5 -> 11; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed illness -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007215:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

- **1953.06** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-printed-as-praise` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Berta Huber 2写了一封告状信，列数Mitzi的懒惰、贪婪和“可疑的出身”，却把信封上的地址写成了报社。Maria Weiss以为这是一篇自荐小品，删掉几处脏话登了出来；读者喜欢这种不留情面的自嘲，两个商行给Mitzi寄来聘书。Berta Huber 2在咖啡馆看见报纸，差点把杯子捏碎。
  _effects: cash 4 (商行聘书的预付薪金); reputation 0.04999999999999999; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1213; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; condition; flag +enemys_letter_became_an_advert; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Luise Lechner抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Luise Lechner用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003123:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Mitzi把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Mitzi就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018655_

  布店柜台前，Mitzi买了一副最便宜的深色手套。Anton Bauer问是不是手冻了，Mitzi说是。此后三个月，Mitzi在院子里洗衣服也戴着它；手套磨破了指尖，印子反倒没有人再提起。
  _effects: cash -1 (一副深色手套); state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 43 -> 33; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.concealed false -> true; flag +df18_mark_hidden_by_glove; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi收到一封没有署名的信，里面是那家旅馆的登记页。信末写着一个数目和一句“月底之前”；Mitzi把钱数了两遍，还是差一点。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 58 -> 73; state_parameter known-affair.grief 15 -> 23; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer)_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/a-strand-snaps` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  一个证人搬走了，带走了他那一份记忆。Mitzi在旧地址门口站了半个下午，回来时发现信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 48 -> 56; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 88 -> 82; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 51 -> 56; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +scandal_witness_left_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Mitzi是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Mitzi在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 60 -> 56; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 50 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Mitzi在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Mitzi低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 33 -> 43; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 11 -> 17; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Mitzi的名字从海报上撤下；Mitzi在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Mitzi是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 10 -> 2; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 4 -> 16; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 2 -> 1; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `r13-work.open-shop::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-vacant-shop/took-the-vacant-lease` tags: commerce, shop, capital, licence, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  战前那家鞋铺没有撑到战后，货架还钉在墙上。Friedrich Weiss把租金压到战前的七成，只要Mitzi肯自己修门面。Mitzi用旧报纸糊了墙，把“鞋”字改成“杂货”，开张那天，整条街的人都来看新招牌。
  _effects: cash -9 (卷帘门上的第一块木牌); flag +work13_took_a_postwar_vacant_shop; role; employment Kaufmann @ counter-monument:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14-evt:world-vienna-1912:017070_

- **1954.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Maria Weiss说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Maria Weiss陪着Mitzi去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.12** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Mitzi转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Marie Huber还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1955.01** `df18-mark.reading-at-market::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/read-as-curse` tags: body, mark, omen, rumor, superstition, market, vienna, urban
  肉摊的Rosa Berg 2看见Mitzi挽袖子，刀停在半空，说这种印子像吊死的人留下的手印。旁边买菜的人安静了一瞬，又各自低头挑菜。Mitzi想笑，却发现自己的手在抖；那天回家，Mitzi绕开了平时走的巷子。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 43 -> 58; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; flag +df18_mark_read_as_curse; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.01** `major-crisis.press-accident/press-kills` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health
  同一台机器在夜班又卡住了。Mitzi伸手去拽纸边，滚筒突然合拢，把人和半截工作服一起拖向齿轮箱。Hermann Winter拉下总闸时，Mitzi的胸口已经塌下去一块。医生蹲在油污的地上写死亡证明，工厂当天下午就换了新的防护栏，工头说订单不能停。
  _effects: death (crushed chest in a printing press); flag -known_affair_active; flag -scandal_web_active; flag +df18_mark_faded; flag -ridiculously_famous_

### 239 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Josef哭了很久，Josef Bauer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josef Bauer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1914.05** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Josef问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Klara在楼道里滚一只铁环，Josef站在门口看。Klara把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1916.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1916.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1916.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1918.04** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-sits-on-the-chair` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Emil一屁股坐在那把空椅子上，说“让我也见见你的朋友”。Josef急得去拉椅子腿，两个人把盘子碰翻在地，土豆滚到灶台下。Josef Bauer赶来时，Josef正趴在炉边找那块土豆，嘴里还在替朋友道歉。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:25; relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03; flag +friend_defended_from_sibling; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1918.09** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Josef把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Josef把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Katharina Bauer接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Emil Weiss从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Josef。Josef把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。

  烟草店的门铃响一次，Josef就往柜台边挪一步。Rosa Moser终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Rosa Moser说完就去招呼别的客人。Josef把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Rosa Moser看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000332:child_

  “你看看这角。”Berta Weiss把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Josef伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Berta Weiss从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Josef手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Josef一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Berta Huber的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Luise Huber忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Josef陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Luise Huber站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.1; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; mood -0.05000000000000002_

  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  户籍官Hermann Weiss 2为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Josef盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Josef收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  一个税务员把Josef的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Josef收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Josef先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。

  _effects: role; reputation 0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; cash -1 (签名纸、墨水和面包店门口的租金); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分开，窗口职员会当面数签名”; flag +petition_filed_

  这一年，Josef学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Josef的材料齐全。年底，Josef从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 36 -> 40; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 42 -> 37; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1938.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef走到Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1938.04** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/apply-for-a-municipal-flat` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Josef去市政办事处登记申请更大的市营住宅。办事员收下表格和两克朗手续费，说前面还有几百户，至少要等两年；Josef把回执折好，回家路上看见新盖的市营楼房，窗户亮着，门却还进不去。
  _effects: cash -2 (municipal housing application); role_

- **1938.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Helene Moser 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1938.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Josef顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (走了很远的碎石路，脚底发疼); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Hermann Winter把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Hermann Winter低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:office-under-siege` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  Josef的「职位受围」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:507_

- **1939.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Ferdinand Berg帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Josef点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:004099; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.01** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-favor` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  牌桌上输掉最后一张钞票后，Luise Hofer把一叠钱推到Josef面前，说不用写借条，只要Josef答应“以后有一天帮一个忙”。Josef数了数，正好二十克朗。Luise Hofer没有说是什么忙，只把烟按灭在桌角，说时候到了会来找Josef。
  _effects: cash 20 (牌桌上借来的二十克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:574; flag +df18_took_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-windfall` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  Josef在路边捡到一枚硬币，或是在旧外套里摸出一张没花完的票子。高兴只持续了很短；Josef随即想起，那本账还在别人手里。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 25 -> 30; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 35 -> 40; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009640_

- **1939.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Elisabeth Huber的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Elisabeth Huber提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1939.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Hermann Winter手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Hermann Winter把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1940.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/motion-fails` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  会议室里有人念了一份动议，说Josef把预算当成人情。Hermann Weiss 2把签名页传了一圈，最后停在自己手里：支持票比反对票少三张。Josef没有发言，只把下一季度的支出表放在桌上，表上每一笔都有收据。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +survived_removal_motion; knowledge “动议只拿到少数支持，三名原本签名的委员在投票前撤回了。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/price-arrives` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  第六个月，债主在楼梯口拦住Josef，说利息不能总按原来的算法。Josef看着对方手里的账本，忽然发现那上面的墨迹比上次新，数字也比上次多。
  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 40 -> 46; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 30 -> 25; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef去了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，坐在后排听完了关于环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsam的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1931 as participant; knowledge “我参加了环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Ga。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1940.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef上楼敲了Franz Winter的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Franz Winter把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Josef能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001521:child_

- **1940.07** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为环城大道写字楼管理处的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001521:child_

- **1941.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-scandal-helps` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  照片登出后，Josef收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Josef去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:618; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/paid-a-little` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  半年过去，Josef还了一点钱，也替债主跑了两趟腿。债主没有夸Josef，只在账本上划掉一小行；Josef却发现，自己开始下意识地绕开那家店。
  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 46 -> 38; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 25 -> 28; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Josef想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1941.04** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/rent-book-rewritten` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Emil Winter在房东的租金簿上把Josef的名字划掉，换成自己的，又把两个人合住的房间说成是Emil Winter一个人租的。房东没有多问，只让Josef月底前搬走。Emil Winter站在门口，手里拿着那本不该由Emil Winter保管的簿子。
  _effects: cash -2 (临时找住处与搬家的费用); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001461:child; reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +rent_book_taken; condition; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1941.04** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010926_

- **1941.07** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Josef没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Josef，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1941.10** `hist15-purge.seed::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/the-file-stays-closed` tags: purge, repression, organization, surveillance, history, work, vienna, urban
  人事科的门开了又关，Josef的档案被放在最下面。没有人解释为什么，只有Ferdinand Winter 2把一叠旧表格推过来，说“先把这个月做完”。窗外的口号声一阵高一阵低，处里的打字机一直没有停。
  _effects: state_add institution-purge state:world-vienna-1912:688; mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “审查名单还没有公开，但人事科已经把所有档案重新排过。”_

- **1942.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Josef在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Katharina Berg第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Emil Lechner发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @morgue:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005591; organization_funds; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000730:child; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1942.03** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  Helene Stein 2把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Josef解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Gustav Stein递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); mood -0.030000000000000138; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

- **1942.06** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/sit-with-the-stain` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  洗衣房的墙上有一块水渍，形状像一个披着斗篷的女人。Josef第一次看见时，正好腰疼得直不起来；她点了一根蜡烛，坐了十分钟，疼没有消失，但呼吸慢了下来。Ferdinand Lechner 2说那是圣母，Josef没有纠正，也没有把水渍擦掉。
  _effects: cash -1 (洗衣房里的一根蜡烛); mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “洗衣房的水渍像一个披斗篷的女人，我在它面前坐了一会儿。”; flag +df18_water_stain_consolation; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1942.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Josef把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Ferdinand Winter 2放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Emil Weiss在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1943.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/appointed-ally` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Josef把Friedrich Kral 2的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Emil Lechner说这个岗位没有编制，Josef说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Friedrich Kral 2带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @morgue:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005591; organization_funds; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

- **1943.01** `r13-edu.numeracy-class::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/catches-short-wage` tags: education, numeracy, adult_education, wages, work, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef在夜校学会算百分比，回家把工钱袋倒在桌上，一笔一笔对。工头少算了三个加班钟点，Josef第二天拿着纸去找Ferdinand Winter 2，Ferdinand Winter 2看了半天，补了两克朗，说以后别在车间里声张。那两克朗Josef没花，夹在账本里，旁边写着“加班三小时”。
  _effects: cash 4 (算盘上的工钱); flag +checked_wage_slip; skill_

- **1943.04** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/municipal-mortuary` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  市立太平间缺一个夜班杂役，Rosa Bauer 2只问Josef怕不怕冷。地下室的抽屉一排十二个，有的拉开了就不再推回去；Josef的工作是登记遗物、给无名尸挂上编号，再在天亮前把地拖干净。
  _effects: organization_found 市立太平间 (state); position 太平间杂役 @morgue:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:012306; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:792; cash 2 (municipal night wages); knowledge “太平间地下室的抽屉一排十二个，无名尸也要有编号。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1943.05** `ld18-bathhouse.vow::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-vow-in-the-steam` tags: love, queer, men, intimacy, secret, vow, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Josef和Friedrich Huber在澡堂最里面的隔间交换了戒指，蒸汽把两个人的脸都熏红了。没有证婚人，只有一只漏水的铜龙头在滴答响；Friedrich Huber说这声音像教堂的钟，Josef说那就当它是。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:793; cash -1 (two rings and the bathhouse fee); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +vow_in_the_steam; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1944.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Josef把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Josef知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Josef已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Josef也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Josef和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Josef在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Anna Moser 4只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1944.07** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; reputation -0.03; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1944.10** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 40 -> 48; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1944.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Josef去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child_

- **1945.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Josef，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Josef说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Josef把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 22 -> 30; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 40 -> 36; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  苏联士兵进城时，Josef躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Josef去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  Josef算了一下，四克朗够一个星期午饭。他改约Klara去教堂听免费的管风琴音乐会，捐了两赫勒，坐在后排的长凳上。回去时两人在街角分吃一个烤土豆。
  _effects: cash -0.06 (教堂捐献与烤土豆); food; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1945.01** `r1-civ.black-market-cafe::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/bought-flour-on-black-market` tags: scarcity, black market, cafe, police, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  咖啡馆后间的桌上放着一袋面粉，袋口用蓝线缝着，Marie Huber用勺子舀出一勺，让Josef看颜色。价钱是官价的三倍，Marie Huber说“嫌贵可以去排队”。Josef数了钱，Ferdinand Winter 2在门口望风，听见门铃响就敲两下墙。
  _effects: cash -12 (咖啡馆后间的面粉袋); food; mood 0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +bought_flour_on_black_market; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; knowledge “the back room is entered through the kitchen and the waiter ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

- **1945.06** `p10-work-career.demotion-dismissal/p10-work-career.demotion-dismissal.transfer` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, demotion
  Josef拿了调令去仓库，工钱少了三成，但班次还在。Ferdinand Winter 2帮他把工具箱从车床边搬走，路过门口时他回头看了一眼自己站过的位置，铁板上还留着一圈油印。月底会计照常把工资袋递过来。
  _effects: employment 仓库杂工 @ building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536; cash -2 (工钱减少后家里开销吃紧); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Josef把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill_

- **1946.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Rosa Bauer 2用Josef留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Josef发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Josef自己的名字。Rosa Bauer 2说只是替Josef省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.09999999999999998; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.07000000000000006_

  Josef在食堂角落上课，桌上还留着上一班的汤渍。Mitzi把车间的废纸订成练习本，Mitzi Lechner用粉笔在墙上写成本核算。Josef算出一批零件的废品率，第二天被工头叫去问是谁教的。工头没有回答，第二天却让Josef把废品率写成黑板报，贴在食堂门口。
  _effects: flag +factory_night_class; skill_

- **1946.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Josef站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

  _effects: cash -3 (破坏暗房的罚款); reputation -0.03999999999999998; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:925; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +destroyed_a_wedding_portrait; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Josef把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Grete 2没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Josef回头看了一眼，Grete 2正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003587:child_

  信过了三个星期被退回来，信封上盖着“查无此人”，边角已经磨毛。Luise Huber不认得那四个字，让Josef念；Josef念成“还在路上”，把信折好放进抽屉。Luise Huber点点头，第二天又问有没有回信。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +letter_returned_undeliverable; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  床架从楼梯上拖下来时油漆蹭掉了好几块，Josef站在旁边没作声。收破烂的走后，屋里空得回声都变大了，Josef抱着铁盒出门，只把门锁好，钥匙从看门人手里交出去。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1947.12** `p21-returned-enemy::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/the-old-letter` tags: enemy, return, betrayal, revenge, debt, blacklist, history, public
  Gustav Lechner没有拿出借据，而是拿出一封信，信上是Josef年轻时写给别人的一句话。他说不要钱，只要Josef在下次委员会上投一个名字；Josef把信看完，发现纸角有一处被水浸过的痕迹，像多年前就已经准备好了。
  _effects: state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:977; cash -3 (the first installment on the old debt); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000099:child; flag +old_enemy_blackmail; knowledge “信纸的角上有一处被水浸过的痕迹，像多年前就已经准备好了。”; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +scandal_web_active_

  背叛发生以后，Josef最先想到的不是损失，而是那个人知道多少。第二天，对方在街角点了点头，像在提醒Josef：现在两个人都有东西可以卖了。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 30 -> 22; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 70 -> 78; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 45 -> 50; flag +scandal_web_betrayed_

  Katharina Bauer 2的母亲病重，家里要她回去嫁给一个开杂货铺的人。Katharina Bauer 2来告别时，Josef替她把箱子提到车站；两个人在月台上站到铃响，谁也没有说“等我”。火车开走后，Josef回到那两间房，发现隔壁的钥匙还插在门上。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:793; health -0.020000000000000018 (sleepless nights after the departure); flag +left_for_a_marriage; flag -impossible_attachment_active_

  _effects: cash -2 (星期六下午工钱按日扣减); health 0.020000000000000018 (终于有半天空闲); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home

  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.030000000000000027 (crowded sleeping space); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child_

- **1949.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/keep-it-for-the-children` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  Emil想把钱拿去盘下街角的小酒馆，Josef却把十五克朗直接交到学校，替孩子续了下一年的学费。Emil把餐巾摔在桌上，说这家人永远只会把钱埋进土里。Emil Lechner 3没有劝，只把汤碗往Josef那边推了推。
  _effects: cash -15 (school fees paid from the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 20 -> 5; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 47 -> 59; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 5; flag +fortune_kept_for_child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/a-strand-snaps` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  一个证人搬走了，带走了他那一份记忆。Josef在旧地址门口站了半个下午，回来时发现信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 58 -> 66; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 78 -> 72; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 51 -> 56; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +scandal_witness_left_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/mocked-by-younger-students` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef被年轻同学笑说口音重，念课文时全班哄笑。Josef把书合上，第二天没来。Mitzi Lechner托人带话，Josef只说家里忙，其实每天那个时辰都坐在河边，用树枝在泥地上划自己的名字。河水涨起来的那天，Josef在泥地上写的名字被冲掉了，Josef又写了一遍。
  _effects: skill_

- **1949.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family, injury
  Josef和Ferdinand Berg 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Josef去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Josef先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.019999999999999997 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1949.09** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Josef在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.12** `r1-cul.fashion-purchase::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ruined-by-rain` tags: culture, fashion, shop, money, vanity, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在橱窗前站了三个下午，最后数出十二克朗，把帽子捧回家。第一次戴出去就下雨，帽檐塌下来，像一只湿透的鸟；Emil Lechner 3把自己的旧毡帽换给Josef，说这顶经得起雨。Josef把新帽子晾在炉边，第二天缩了一圈，再也戴不上，十二克朗只剩下一圈硬边。
  _effects: cash -12 (橱窗里的帽子); condition; flag +hat_ruined_by_rain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-135_

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015761_

- **1950.01** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/the-committee-buys-a-wagon` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  捐款交出去三个月，诊所还没有开门，先来了一辆新的弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。Luise Moser 2把账本摊在桌上，说委员会认为“体面的门面”也是诊所的一部分。Berta Hofer在门口骂了一句，Josef把账本翻到最后一页，发现自己捐的钱只剩一半。
  _effects: cash -30 (the clinic endowment was spent on furniture and a wagon); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; condition; flag +clinic_funds_misused; knowledge “诊所的捐款先买了一辆弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/a-strand-snaps` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  一个证人搬走了，带走了他那一份记忆。Josef在旧地址门口站了半个下午，回来时发现信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 66 -> 74; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 72 -> 66; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 56 -> 61; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/settle-the-account` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  两个星期不来往，门口碰见时Josef和Ferdinand Lechner 2都先看别处。这次Josef先开口，把账本里的一页撕下来摊在柜台上，说数字是自己写错的；Ferdinand Lechner 2看了半天，说下回别在酒馆里算账。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一小束紫罗兰和两只 Semmel); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1950.01** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Anton谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Anton说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Anna Lechner 4听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1950.08** `pl19-bridge.public-rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-list-writes-itself` tags: scandal, protection, network, press, media, rumor, bridge, vienna
  Josef在报纸上看见自己的名字和一张旧照片，报纸像一盏关不掉的灯。Katharina Bauer 2提议找几个人替Josef挡电话、挡记者、挡亲戚；Josef说不用，第二天却发现Katharina Bauer 2已经把名单写好了，最上面一行是Josef自己。
  _effects: state_add pl19-protected-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:1104; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +list_written_itself; knowledge “名单最上面一行是当事人的名字。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +protected_scandal_active_

- **1950.08** `state_reaction:pl19-protected-scandal/a-public-question` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  有人在一场公开活动里问起Josef的“私人安排”，语气客气，问题却像一根针。Josef笑着答了别的事，回家后才发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 55 -> 67; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 40 -> 34; reputation -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017225_

- **1950.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Josef把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; flag +union_ledger_public_

- **1951.01** `p21-scandal-auction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-letters-are-read-aloud` tags: blackmail, auction, scandal, letters, public, secret, humiliation, vienna
  Luise Moser 2没有等到最后一轮竞价。他说“委托人希望公开”，把第七号信封拆开，念了第一页；Emil Lechner 3在第二排站起来，椅子腿在木地板上刮出一声长响。Luise Stein没有笑，只把拍卖目录合上；Josef听见自己的名字被念了两次，第二次比第一次轻。
  _effects: state_transition p21-scandal-web -> public-scandal; reputation -0.07999999999999996; flag +letters_read_at_auction; knowledge “第一页念完时，伴侣在第二排站起来，椅子腿刮出一声长响。”; cash -1 (the auction admission and a cab home); mood -0.06999999999999995; flag -scandal_web_active_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017223_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:pl19-protected-scandal/the-network-meets/network-holds` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  Josef又在后间见了那几个人。谁也没有提名字，只把下个月的班次、钥匙和该打的电话分了一遍；出门时，Josef发现自己的围巾被人重新叠过，像一件被妥善保管的证物。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 60 -> 55; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 34 -> 38; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 67 -> 65; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Anna Stein 3把一把小钥匙放在咖啡杯旁边，说旅馆后门的房间按周租，不会有人问名字。Josef把钥匙收进内袋，指尖碰到里面的车票和家门钥匙。两人没有多坐，出门时一前一后，Josef在街角回头，看见Anna Stein 3还站在灯下。
  _effects: cash -3 (旅馆后门的第二把钥匙); mood 0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +affair_started; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  那个替Josef保守秘密的人，在另一个场合把话说漏了半句。没有人当场追问，但第二天，Josef发现后间的门没有关严。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 55 -> 65; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 10 -> 18; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.publicHeat 65 -> 69; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.03** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Helene Lechner提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005526:child_

- **1951.07** `r1-work.promotion-rival::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/declined-the-badge` tags: work, promotion, rivalry, foreman, factory, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把任命书推回去，说Anton的班次排得不对，自己上去也是挨骂。Anton愣了一下，把纸收进抽屉。Anna Bauer在门口听见，第二天给Josef带了一包烟，烟盒上还沾着车间的油。
  _effects: flag +declined_promotion; role; relation friend with person-anna-bauer_

- **1951.07** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `r14-fam.coat-check::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ticket-in-the-pocket` tags: family, marriage, forbidden_affair, betrayal, discovery, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Lechner 3洗外套时从口袋里摸出两张衣帽间的号牌，号码不是同一晚的。Josef回家时，桌上摆着那两张硬纸片，Anna Stein 3的围巾搭在椅背上。Emil Lechner 3没有提高声音，只问要不要把箱子先收好；Josef看见窗外的雪落在消防梯上，一直没有化。
  _effects: cash -3 (衣帽间的号牌); mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.09999999999999998; flag +affair_discovered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017604_

- **1952.01** `ds17-blackmail.debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/the-landlord-with-a-copy` tags: love, scandal, blackmail, crime, money, secret_intimacy, kept_secret, vienna
  Emil Lechner 3原来是Josef的房东代理人。他没有提高嗓门，只把下季度的房租单和一张照片放在一起；Josef这才明白，勒索可以穿得很体面。Emil Lechner提议搬家，Josef算了算搬家的钱，决定先忍到春天。
  _effects: cash -4 (the raised rent and a copy of the photograph); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; reputation -0.020000000000000018; state_transition public-scandal -> ds17-kept-secret; flag +landlord_blackmail; flag +kept_secret_active_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:pl19-protected-scandal/the-network-meets/someone-wants-a-favor` tags: pl19-protected-scandal, love, secret, scandal, power, protection, network, protected_scandal
  有人替Josef挡了一次采访，随后在走廊里提起自己儿子的学徒期满。Josef知道这不是闲聊；当天晚上，那张写着名字的纸条被压在糖罐下面，像一枚没有付清的硬币。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 18 -> 30; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 38 -> 34; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 65 -> 70_

- **1952.01** `r13-fam.take-child-to-school::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/school-refused` tags: task, family, school, child, enrolment, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Lechner翻到报名纸背面，说还缺一张出生证明和一双完整的鞋。Josef低头看Grete 3脚上那双露脚趾的鞋，说下周补上；Mitzi Lechner把纸推回来，说名额只留到星期五。回家的路上，Grete 3一直问学校是什么样子。
  _effects: cash -1 (书包带系紧一点); flag +school_enrolment_refused; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:013736:child; condition_

- **1952.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Katharina Winter说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Josef站在原地看着Katharina Winter拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1952.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Josef额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Gustav Bauer 2坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1952.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Josef嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Josef用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把Anna Moser 4和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Josef把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1953.01** `r14-fam.court-corridor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/separation-granted` tags: family, marriage, divorce, institution, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  窗口里的办事员把两张表格推到长凳上，说财产各归各的，孩子的事另写一张。Josef在签名处停了一会儿，Emil Lechner 3已经先签了，笔迹比平时用力。走廊尽头的窗子开着，风把复写纸吹得翻了一页；两人一前一后下楼，没有走同一段楼梯。
  _effects: cash -6 (法院走廊里的长凳); flag +divorced; role; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  Josef又把针线盒锁好，把钥匙换了地方。这一年里Josef照常去市场、照常替邻居收信，甚至在圣灵降临节做了两次弥撒；只有深夜清点抽屉时，Josef才会把那张纸拿出来看一眼，再放回去。
  _effects: state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.weight 50 -> 53; state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.exposure 30 -> 27; state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.protection 10 -> 12; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/collected-forty-kroner` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Katharina Winter把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Josef把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001895:child; organization_funds_

- **1953.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Mitzi Weiss在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1953.11** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/parrot-dies` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, vienna
  鹦鹉死在第二个冬天。窗缝里灌进来的风把笼布吹开，它缩在食盆旁边，像一团被揉皱的灰纸。Anna Weiss说条件没有完成，十二克朗归了姑妈的教子；Josef把鸟埋在院子里，留下那只笼子。春天，笼子里长出一丛野草，Josef没有拔。
  _effects: cash -2 (鹦鹉的药费和笼布); reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; condition; flag +parrot_died_before_the_will; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Berta Hofer从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002136:child_

- **1953.12** `r1-cul.libel-threat::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop::r1-cul.libel-threat-reform/one-krone-damages` tags: culture, newspaper, law, reputation, evidence, vienna, urban, postwar
  改革派律师主动来找Josef，说愿意免费代理。开庭那天，旁听席坐满记者；Emil在最后陈述里念了Josef的报道原文。判决下来，赔偿只有一克朗，Josef把判决书贴在编辑部墙上，旁边是那张一克朗的邮票。
  _effects: cash 1 (律师信上的第二段); reputation 0.09999999999999998; flag +won_symbolic_libel_case; role; knowledge “the court awarded one krone in damages”_

- **1954.01** `r14-fam.sunday-suitcase::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/child-refuses` tags: family, divorce, children, co_parenting, custody, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Lechner抓着门框，说今天不想去。Gustav Lechner蹲下来哄了很久，最后把苹果放在门槛上，一个人走了。Josef把苹果洗干净，切成四块；Helene Lechner只吃了一块，剩下的放在窗台上，看着它们慢慢变黄。
  _effects: condition; flag +child_refused_handover; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000099:child; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008376:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018408_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ds17-kept-secret/one-more-year/the-secret-shows` tags: ds17-kept-secret, love, secret, shame, family, risk, kept_secret, body
  Josef在洗衣房晕了一会儿，醒来时手里还攥着别人的床单。女人们围过来，有人递水，有人问是不是又有了；Josef说只是没吃早饭，声音却比平时高了半度。下午，整栋楼都在猜Josef最近为什么不再穿那件收腰的外套。
  _effects: state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.exposure 27 -> 37; state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.weight 53 -> 58; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/certificate-by-post` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef每周收到一叠油印讲义，做完题再寄回去。Mitzi Lechner在页边用红笔改错，有时附一句“第二步再想想”。十二个月后，Josef收到结业证书，纸很薄，但编号印得清楚。证书被Josef钉在床头，邮差再来时，问Josef要不要报下一门课。编号是手写的。
  _effects: flag +correspondence_certificate; skill_

- **1954.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把通知念给Ferdinand Berg听，Ferdinand Berg摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.06** `major-crisis.press-accident/light-duty` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health, injury
  离合器救下了Josef的大半只手，但左手的两根指头被压扁，骨头长好以后也弯不回来。工厂把Josef调到校对台，工资降到八十克朗；事故保险只认“部分丧失劳动能力”，赔了十二克朗。Gustav Moser 3替Josef把工具柜搬下楼，谁都没提那台机器为什么没有护罩。
  _effects: health -0.12 (left hand crushed by the press); condition; employment Buchdrucker (Schonposten) @ building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536; cash 12 (partial disability payment from the accident insurer); role; flag +light_duty; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151_

- **1954.06** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/denounced-to-police` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, injury, unemployment
  旅店伙计记下了Josef和Alois Weiss的名字。几天后警察来敲门，把两人带去问话；Josef丢了工位，罚了八克朗，Alois Weiss的家里把人接走。Josef在档案里留下一条警察记录，手指上还留着问话时磕出的淤青。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003174:child; health -0.05999999999999998 (police questioning); reputation -0.19999999999999996; unemployment (dismissed after police visit); cash -8 (fine and lost work)_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Berta Hofer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Berta Hofer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019334_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:ds17-kept-secret/one-more-year/the-secret-shows` tags: ds17-kept-secret, love, secret, shame, family, risk, kept_secret, body
  Josef在洗衣房晕了一会儿，醒来时手里还攥着别人的床单。女人们围过来，有人递水，有人问是不是又有了；Josef说只是没吃早饭，声音却比平时高了半度。下午，整栋楼都在猜Josef最近为什么不再穿那件收腰的外套。
  _effects: state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.exposure 37 -> 47; state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.weight 58 -> 63_

- **1955.01** `r13-work.deliver-goods::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/delivered-on-time` tags: commerce, delivery, transport, weather, bribe, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把十二只木箱码在板车上，用麻绳捆了两道，轮子碾过石子路时，箱角互相磕着响。走到第三道街口，车轴响了一声，Luise Moser 2蹲下去紧了紧销子。货送到时比约定早半个钟头，店主验完封条，在送货单上画了押。
  _effects: cash 6 (板车上的十二箱); flag +work13_delivered_goods_on_time; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1955.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Josef就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Ferdinand Weiss摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.04999999999999999 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003654:child_

- **1955.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Klara在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1955.12** `p21-mistaken-identity::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-photograph-is-wrong` tags: mistaken_identity, police, identity, arrest, public, secret, absurd, vienna
  Maria Weiss把通缉照片和Josef的脸并排放在灯下，发现照片上的人左眉有一道疤，而Josef没有。Ferdinand Berg 4在旁边作证，说那天晚上Josef一直在帮自己搬煤；Maria Weiss把银怀表推回来，表盖内侧的名字却不是Josef的。灯罩里有一只死蛾。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +mistaken_identity_cleared; knowledge “通缉照片上的人左眉有一道疤，而当事人没有；表盖内侧的名字也不是当事人的。”; cash 1 (compensation for a night in custody); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170_

- **1956.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020037_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:ds17-kept-secret/one-more-year/held-another-year` tags: ds17-kept-secret, love, secret, shame, family, risk, kept_secret, body
  Josef又把针线盒锁好，把钥匙换了地方。这一年里Josef照常去市场、照常替邻居收信，甚至在圣灵降临节做了两次弥撒；只有深夜清点抽屉时，Josef才会把那张纸拿出来看一眼，再放回去。
  _effects: state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.weight 63 -> 66; state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.exposure 47 -> 44; state_parameter ds17-kept-secret.protection 12 -> 14; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/forgiven` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在院子的水龙头边等到Gustav Stein，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Gustav Stein拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Josef问要怎样才能收回，Gustav Stein说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; flag +apology_accepted; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; knowledge “the insult has already reached three courtyards”_

- **1956.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Luise 4这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021576_

- **1956.07** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Katharina Winter却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Josef把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1956.09** `ml18-luck.wrong-horse::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-horse-is-disqualified` tags: gambling, luck, money, horse_race, absurd, windfall, vienna, urban
  马冲过终点，Josef的票已经举到半空；广播里却说骑师过磅轻了半公斤，成绩作废。Anna Lechner把票收回去，在背面盖了一个“无效”的章，又给Josef倒了一杯李子酒。Alois Weiss说至少酒是免费的；Josef喝了一口，觉得比输钱还难受。
  _effects: cash -5 (the losing stake on a disqualified horse); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1337; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005000:child; flag +horse_disqualified; knowledge “那匹马第一个冲线，但骑师过磅轻了半公斤。”; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.09** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Josef上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Josef把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -10 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 20 -> 15; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021390_

- **1956.09** `r1-fam.neighbor-noise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/noise.water-valve` tags: social, neighbor, noise, water, dispute, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef忍了半年，最后把一盆水泼在Helene Lechner的窗台上。第二天，Helene Lechner把总水阀拧小了一半，整层楼的水都细得像线。两家在走廊里吵到中午，Emil Winter来了，各打五十大板：晚上十点以后不许敲，水阀修好，费用两家分摊。
  _effects: cash -2 (半夜的锤子); condition; flag +water_valve_retaliation; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005526:child; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001461:child_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Berta Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 42 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021583_

- **1957.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Josef的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Josef家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021289_

- **1957.01** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-clinic-opens` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  诊所设在街角一间改过的铺面里，门口挂着一块红砖色的木牌。Emil每周来两个下午，给孩子量体重、给老人听肺；Friedrich Weiss送来一盆天竺葵，Anton Weiss把候诊室的长凳刨平。Josef没有剪彩，只把第一张药方折好，放进抽屉最里面。
  _effects: cash -30 (endowment for the neighborhood clinic); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; organization_found 街角诊所 (other); reputation 0.09999999999999998; flag +clinic_opened; knowledge “街角诊所的第一张药方锁在抽屉里，药是给孩子退烧的。”; mood 0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 40 -> 35; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 44 -> 49; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing 0 -> -8; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 15 -> 23; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor)_
