### 268 subject events

- **1912.07** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-bread-pap` tags: infancy, food, household, injury
  奶水不够，Rosa Lechner把黑面包掰碎泡在热水里，加一点糖喂给Rosa。孩子夜里哭得更久，肚子鼓胀。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (bread pap instead of milk); condition; cash -1 (bread and sugar)_

- **1913.05** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/bruised-arm` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger, injury
  Rosa在楼梯口追一只滚下去的线轴，脚下一滑，胳膊先撞在铁扶手上。Alois Lechner听见哭声跑出来，用冷水毛巾敷住那块青紫，嘴里说着“没事”，手却一直在抖。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (fell at the stairwell); condition; mood -0.03_

- **1913.09** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dog-gets-the-first-word` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa追着炉边的狗爬了半间屋，忽然拍着地板喊出狗的名字。狗吓得钻到床下，Alois Lechner笑得把水壶都放下了；邻居在门外问出了什么事。那天晚上，家里给狗多留了半块面包。
  _effects: mood 0.04; food; flag +first_word_dog_name_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Rosa把空碗推回Rosa Lechner面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Rosa Lechner愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.030000000000000002_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Rosa问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Gustav Lechner在楼道里滚一只铁环，Rosa站在门口看。Gustav Lechner把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000099:child; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1917.01** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Rosa一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Alois Lechner听见哭声跑出来，把Rosa抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Rosa哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Rosa弯腰去追，再抬头时Gustav Lechner已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Gustav Berg把孩子抱进灶间，Rosa跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Alois Lechner回来没问经过，只把Rosa的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1918.06** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/ignored-mother-call` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  “再提一桶！”Alois Lechner的声音从灶台边追下来，Rosa把门在身后带上，脚步没停。傍晚回家，水缸底只剩一层浑水。Alois Lechner把扁担往地上一放，Rosa挑着两只桶在院子和井台之间走了四趟，肩膀磨得发烫。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (傍晚被罚挑水四趟); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Maria Berg从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Rosa。Rosa把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Rosa站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Josef Lechner睡醒哭了一声，Rosa把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Alois Lechner回来时，Rosa正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000290:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (提水抬煤累得手臂发酸); reputation -0.03; mood -0.039999999999999994_

  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1921.06** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home

  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06_

  Rosa只把Elisabeth Bauer的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Rosa是不是要请人喝咖啡；Rosa说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。

- **1926.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Rosa把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Otto Lechner看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Rosa没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Rosa才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: flag +helped_sibling_with_homework; role; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child; skill_

- **1926.05** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Klara一样的白手套；Rosa站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 37; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1926.06** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Klara写走了。Rosa在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Klara说没关系，把手放回Rosa背后；第三支曲子响起时，Anton在门口冲Rosa比了个手势，Rosa假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 37 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 35; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04_

- **1926.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Mitzi Lechner说该自己走，Rosa说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Rosa摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: mood -0.07; health -0.030000000000000027 (在冰冷的楼梯上坐到半夜)_

- **1926.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Rosa追下车，把那只手套递还给Klara。Klara说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Rosa回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:123; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Klara。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 35 -> 43; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.03_

- **1927.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-improved` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Otto Lechner把算术本摊在厨房桌上，Rosa用豆子摆出十以内的加减。Otto Lechner算错时，Rosa不骂，只把豆子重新排一遍。两个月后，Otto Lechner的听写和算术都及格了，Alois Lechner在饭桌上多给Rosa盛了半碗汤。
  _effects: flag +tutored_sibling_well; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child_

- **1927.01** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Stein看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Alois Lechner求了半天，Mitzi Stein答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Mitzi Lechner干了三天就走了，Rosa留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Mitzi Stein才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1927.11** `r3-teen.first-move-away::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-workers-dormitory/room-near-work` tags: housing, leaving_home, lodging, work, independence, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在工厂后街租了一个床位，和两个车工合住。房东太太每天收一次煤钱，洗脸水要自己下楼打。第一夜，他听着隔壁的机器声睡着，梦里还在数工票上的尺寸。天没亮，隔壁的车工就敲门叫他一起上工。
  _effects: cash -3 (行李放在楼梯口); mood 0.04; flag +moved_to_lodging_near_work; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; move place-wien-printshop-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1928.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Rosa翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Josef Lechner在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Rosa翻回来时，Gustav Lechner把一支烟塞进Rosa嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 43 -> 58; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; health -0.030000000000000027 (cold and fear on the bridge); reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.05_

  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Klara。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 58 -> 66; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Rosa背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Rosa回家时Otto Lechner还躺着，Rosa把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Otto Lechner自己坐起来端碗，Rosa才觉得肩膀没那么沉。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1928.04** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Rosa把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Alois Lechner发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Rosa都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1929.01** `agency:seek_work` tags: agency, agency:seek_work
  Rosa去了Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne问工，得到的回答都是“下个月再来”。
  _effects: mood -0.04_

- **1929.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Rosa手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.06 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1929.07** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Rosa去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1929.11** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.bad-blood` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Ferdinand Huber的母亲把Rosa带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Rosa把它抱在胸前。Ferdinand Huber站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Rosa把戒指留在门房，走回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring)_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Rosa在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Ferdinand Huber的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1930.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.accepted` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  午休的哨子刚响，Hermann Winter夹着烟走到门口。Rosa报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood 0.05000000000000001_

- **1930.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  柜台上摊着两张工资袋，一张油墨新，一张已经磨毛了边。Rosa把两袋并排放，掌柜伸出食指把旧的拨到一边，说“你管好自己的”。师兄在窗口看街对面修鞋摊，一直没回头。
  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; skill_

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction
  Ferdinand Huber没有答应，只说现在不合适。Rosa把这件事收回去，之后见面仍点头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004107_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Rosa把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Rosa读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/settle-at-the-tavern` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  没有签字，陪对方去酒馆把账当面谈清。掌柜的把欠条一张张摆在桌上，一共二十八克朗。有人替着说了几句好话，讲明利息怎么算、按月还多少，请掌柜的宽限到月底。谈下来先还五克朗，余下的立了新据，担保人一栏空着，掌柜的临走时瞪了一眼。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (酒馆里替对方先垫的第一笔款子和两杯酒); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1931.03** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa把帽子捏在手里上台，说救济表格要填三张、盖章两处，煤却今晚就见底。年轻人拍椅子背，Rosa Kral在门口皱着眉。主持人记下赞成与反对的人数，纸板上的字越写越小。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; mood 0.03_

- **1931.08** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/burn-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Rosa在厨房的炉子里烧掉名单。纸灰卷起来，像黑色的叶子。Rosa仍然去上工，但从那以后不再在公开场合提起Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; mood -0.03_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Rosa不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Rosa拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Rosa的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Rosa和Rosa Bauer 2在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Rosa Bauer 2把煤钱推过来，Rosa接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.03_

- **1932.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  天色还灰着，Rosa手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Josef Weiss让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:004099; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; mood 0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1932.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa正要把空床许给Ferdinand Winter 2，Mitzi Winter却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Rosa回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Ferdinand Winter 2，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  公证人把一封厚信推到桌子中央：Rosa的叔父在第二区留下一间小印刷所、一台坏掉的压机和十四克朗的欠账。Alois Lechner说那台压机早该当废铁，但订阅名单上还有三百个名字。Rosa把名单卖给对街的印厂，替叔父还了债，剩下的钱刚好够换一套冬衣。
  _effects: cash 12 (sale of the print shop subscription list); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:308; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; skill; flag +inherited_print_shop; mood 0.039999999999999994; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.03_

- **1932.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Friedrich Huber提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Rosa把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  Otto Lechner从考场出来，手里攥着准考证，说最后一道题做出来了。放榜那天，Rosa陪Otto Lechner去看榜，名字在中间偏下，够了。Otto Lechner跳起来，又立刻站好，回头问Rosa能不能把旧课本留下。报名费两克朗，Rosa替Otto Lechner交了。
  _effects: cash -2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_passed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child_

  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Rosa的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/refuse-to-sign` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  把那张字据推回去，说房租和母亲的药费都在桌上摆着，这名字不能签。前两次的借条从证件夹里翻出来放在一起，摊平让对方看清。对方把纸卷起来收进外套，门带得比平常重，此后在街上碰见只当没认出来。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1933.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Rosa站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1933.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.05; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1933.12** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Rosa Bauer 2在午休时把本子摊在Rosa的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Rosa从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Rosa Bauer 2当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; reputation 0.04_

- **1934.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.quit-job` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  Rosa把半天工的工钱单交回去，换回自己看孩子。晚饭从两顿并成一顿，土豆和面包皮煮一锅，伴侣把煤钱数了两遍。孩子睡下后，Rosa在窗边缝袜子，一枚针扎了手指，她含住指头继续缝。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入); food; health -0.040000000000000036 (营养不足和夜里缝补的劳累)_

- **1934.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Rosa当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债); health -0.030000000000000027 (母亲的药钱被挪去还债，家里几夜没睡好); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  姐姐家在多瑙河对岸，院子里晾着床单。孩子在那里住了一个月，回来时脸圆了些，手里攥着一块河边的白石子。可他站在门口不肯叫Rosa，姐姐蹲下来哄，Rosa把行李提进屋，听见身后孩子叫了一声姨。
  _effects: mood -0.1; relation kin with person-franz-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1936.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Rosa手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1936.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  Rosa说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Friedrich Hofer 2一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (高烧硬熬伤身); condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1937.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.deposit-refused` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  “十克朗押金，先交后干。”Mitzi Lechner 2说得很平。Rosa把两只口袋翻给人家看，柜台上只落下一张皱车票。工头收回话头，他也不再问，提着帽子走出栅栏。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +seeking_work; reputation -0.01; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1937.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/refuse-push-bill` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa把药费单从糖罐底下抽出来，对折，推回桌子中央，说上个月的工钱已经贴过煤钱，这回一个赫勒也拿不出。Franz Lechner的脸沉下来，练习本翻得哗哗响，最后把单子重新压回糖罐底下，起身去够门后的外套。三只咖啡杯里的渣子凉在桌上，兄妹这一回把话都说薄了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1937.08** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1937.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1937.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Hermann Weiss 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Rosa排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/violets-on-the-step` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; mood -0.03_

- **1938.02** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Friedrich Kral怀里的孩子脸烧得通红，Rosa往后退了半步，把用粉笔写的号码塞进Friedrich Kral手里。药房伙计收了六十赫勒，给一瓶褐色药水；回家路上Rosa一阵阵发冷。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1938.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Rosa没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1938.08** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Rosa在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Rosa去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为环城大道写字楼管理处的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  Rosa在Hermann Weiss 2面前签下放弃继承的声明，Alois Lechner从内袋里摸出二十克朗推过来，纸币上还带着体温。Mitzi Lechner把房契往自己那边挪了挪。Rosa数也没数就把钱收好，出门时听见公证人办公室的门在背后合上，铜把手凉了一下手心，这二十克朗把兄妹之间的话买断了一截。
  _effects: cash 20 (放弃继承换来一笔现钱); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +renounced_inheritance_

- **1939.01** `kernel-social.reconciliation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/reconciliation.shared-pot` tags: task, task:social, social, reconciliation, family, apology, conflict, vienna
  Rosa端着一锅炖肉去敲Mitzi Lechner的门，说肉炖多了，一个人吃不完。Mitzi Lechner让他进屋，把两只盘子摆在桌上；两人先谈煤价，再谈母亲的坟，最后才提那笔说不清的钱。Gustav Bauer 3在楼下等消息，等到灯灭才走。
  _effects: cash -2 (meat and coal for the reconciliation supper); role; flag +family_reconciled; knowledge “两人把煤价、母亲的坟和那笔钱都谈到了。”_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:576; reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food_

- **1939.08** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-favor` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna

  _effects: cash 20 (牌桌上借来的二十克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:577; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; flag +df18_took_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

  这一年，Rosa学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Rosa的材料齐全。年底，Rosa从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。

- **1941.01** `bc18-ghost.quiet-correction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/school-inspector` tags: bureaucracy, paperwork, identity, absurd, legal, quiet, comic, vienna
  学务督察按着新户籍找上门，说有一名适龄儿童从未入学。Rosa躲在阁楼里，Rosa Weiss 2在楼下说孩子出疹子，不能见风；督察留下了一张入学通知和一瓶鱼肝油。晚上，Rosa从阁楼下来，看见通知上写着“七岁”，忽然觉得自己这一生确实该重新开始。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 30; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; condition; flag +school_notice_for_newborn; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Rosa刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Rosa站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 30 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 1 -> 2; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 1 -> 2; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010262_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Rosa学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Rosa的材料齐全。年底，Rosa从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 29 -> 33; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/price-arrives` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  第六个月，债主在楼梯口拦住Rosa，说利息不能总按原来的算法。Rosa看着对方手里的账本，忽然发现那上面的墨迹比上次新，数字也比上次多。
  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 36 -> 42; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 33 -> 28; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.quit-job` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  厂里说半天工没了就没了，Rosa没再争。厨房里少了一只面包，晚饭只有一锅糊糊。伴侣把勺子放下，说来年春天再说。夜里Rosa替邻居补袜子，三个赫勒一双，缝到灯芯烧短。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1941.02** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Rosa在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.02_

- **1941.08** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  收破烂的把两克朗数在门槛上，顺手敲了敲桌板看木料。Rosa把铁盒扣好，那里面只有照片和几封旧信。Alois Lechner 2替Rosa扶着门，说旧床架值不了几个钱，能脱手就不亏。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1941.12** `r13-turn.inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/estate-settled` tags: law, inheritance, death, property, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  办事员把遗产清单念了一遍：一间铺面、三件工具、二十四克朗现金。家里另一个孩子没有到场，只寄来一封信，说铺面该归Rosa，工具另算。Rosa在清单末尾签了字，把Alois Lechner的旧围裙从铺面里拿出来，叠好带回家。
  _effects: condition; flag +inheritance_settled; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child; cash 24 (inheritance from the parent’s shop savings after the estate was settled); death (老年病去世)_

  教堂抽奖在堂区大厅举行，Franz Bauer把奖券塞进Rosa手里。Rosa抽中了一只烟熏火腿，抱回家分给楼道里的四户人。大家都说Rosa最近运气好；Rosa笑着应了，夜里却把剩下的骨头收进锅里，煮了三天的汤。
  _effects: food; reputation 0.02; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 42 -> 47; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 28 -> 31; flag +df18_won_ham; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  她的申请被退回，理由是“担保人收入不足”。Emil Lechner说可以找第二担保人；她翻遍通讯录，发现能签字的人不是已经走了，就是不肯再担保。回家后，她把通讯录上能签字的名字一个个划掉。
  _effects: flag +emigration_papers_refused; cash -2 (照片、表格和往返车钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; reputation -0.01; knowledge “护照申请需要担保人收入证明，找不到第二担保人就办不下去”_

- **1942.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Anton在午休时把本子摊在Rosa的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Rosa从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Anton当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.039999999999999994_

- **1942.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Luise Stein说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Rosa站在原地看着Luise Stein拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002189:child; flag +uncertain_courtship_

  战时的公墓不再一口一口地卖地，市政厅说阵亡者和贫民一起葬。Rosa去领骨灰，Josef给了一只小木盒，说编号对不上，可能不是一个人的。Rosa把盒子抱回家，放在桌上；Mitzi Lechner说这样也好，省了棺材钱。
  _effects: cash -2 (棺材铺的账（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.2; flag +buried_in_a_municipal_mass_grave; role; knowledge “公墓无法把骨灰对到具体姓名。”_

  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Rosa嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Rosa用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; reputation 0.04_

  Rosa没有立刻答话，先去敲Maria Kral 3的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Maria Kral 3提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Rosa把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。

  Rosa请同住的Rosa Winter 2跑一趟，把Gustav Weiss约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Gustav Weiss说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Rosa把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。

  Maria Kral 2半夜敲墙，说同屋的人烧得说胡话。Rosa隔着板壁问要不要去叫医生，Maria Kral 2说医生来了也只给退热粉，还要收出诊费。天亮时，楼梯间多了一股石灰水味，Josef把白线画到了隔壁门口。

  Rosa把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Hermann Weiss扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Rosa把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1945.09** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social

  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1945.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/turn-away-with-fare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa直说家里腾不出地方，从抽屉里摸出半克朗车费和一块黑面包，塞给Hermann Winter 2。Hermann Winter 2的藤箱提手已经磨毛，在门槛上磕了一下。Rosa关上门，听见脚步声沿楼梯往下，看门人在楼下问了一句，Hermann Winter 2答得含含糊糊；厨房里少了一块面包，也少了一个本该住下的人。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费); food; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Emil Weiss 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Emil Weiss 2敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1945.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Rosa顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000004 (走了很远的碎石路，脚底发疼); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa把Rosa Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/housing-relative.lodged-elsewhere` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  Rosa在楼里替教父Anton Lechner 2问了一圈，最后在洗衣妇家找到一张空床，每月三克朗。Anton Lechner 2把箱子搬过去，Rosa替他垫了第一个月的房租；两人在楼梯口分开，Anton Lechner 2说等找到工就还。床板短了一截，箱子只能竖着放。
  _effects: cash -3 (first month’s bed rent for the relative); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; role; role; flag +relative_lodged_elsewhere; knowledge “洗衣妇家有一张空床，每月三克朗。”_

- **1946.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  夜里疼醒时，Rosa摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Hermann Lechner 3第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Rosa只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.020000000000000004 (牙病拖延未治); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1946.08** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/move-the-crates` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  梦里，Rosa看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Rosa把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Elisabeth Weiss说Rosa运气好，Rosa没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Anton Lechner 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Anton Lechner 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1946.09** `r3-crime.burglary-lookout::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coughed-a-warning` tags: crime, tenement, police, fear, complicity, vienna, urban, postwar
  楼梯口的灯坏了三层，Rosa抱着伞站在阴影里，听见楼上有人挪动柜子。巷口传来脚步，Rosa咳了一声，Berta Bauer 2从后窗翻下去。Katharina Bauer 4开门倒水，只看见Rosa在系鞋带，说这么晚了还修伞。
  _effects: flag +lookout_warned_burglar; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; knowledge “the burglar left through the back window”_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013699_

- **1947.01** `state_expire:raising-child` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa的「家里有个正在长大的孩子」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:43_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Mitzi Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Hermann Weiss把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); health -0.03 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014797_

- **1947.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa对Anna Stein 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Anna Stein 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1947.07** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.follow-monkey` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  猴子走在她前面，红绳在项圈后面拖着一截。乐师在面包店台阶上坐下，从兜里掏出一块干硬的面包掰成两半，一半给了猴子。他说这条巷子里只有面包店老板娘肯让他靠墙歇脚。
  _effects: skill_

- **1947.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Rosa把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; skill_

  警察局的桌子后面的人把哥哥的名字写进名册，撕下一张编号条。Rosa在走廊里碰到一个女人，她找丈夫，手里也捏着一张条子。两人跑了两处收容所，登记簿翻到最后一页也没有。

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Rosa胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa在告解里承认自己多年来一直恨着父亲。Anton Lechner 2问父亲还活着吗，Rosa说去年死了。Anton Lechner 2让Rosa念一段诗篇，Rosa念到一半哭起来；走出教堂时，雪已经停了，台阶上只有自己的一行脚印。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +confessed_hatred_of_father; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; knowledge “the father died last year and the hatred outlived him”_

  门铃是根铁丝拉的，响得发闷。Mitzi Huber 2提篮进门先看水槽里泡着的碗，再看墙角的煤箱，说价钱她心里有数。Rosa把印泥盒推到桌边，按下去时指尖凉了一下。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1949.09** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Elisabeth Weiss把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Emil Weiss笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Rosa把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +investigated_well_ghost; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; knowledge “the white shape is a petticoat on the plum tree”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1950.01** `kernel-migrate.return-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-return-wait` tags: migration, return, border, family, war, vienna, urban, postwar
  她的入境许可过期两天，Berta Huber 2让她在原车返回和等待审查之间选。她选择等，在边境小屋的硬板床上睡了三个晚上；第四天，许可补好了，她的行李却少了一只。她数了数行李，发现少的是装母亲照片的那只箱子。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -4 (边境小屋的住宿和补办许可); flag +border_refused_then_admitted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; condition; knowledge “入境许可过期可以先等审查，但行李在边境小屋不安全”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Mitzi Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 13 -> 16; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016925_

- **1950.04** `ld18-fitting.measurement::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-mother-sees` tags: love, desire, erotic, craft, queer, fitting, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Katharina Winter的母亲在试衣间外站了很久，忽然进来替女儿拉上帘子。她没有骂人，只把工钱放在桌上，说以后不必再来了。Rosa把钱收好，出门时听见Katharina Winter在里面说“妈”，声音像被布料闷住了。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:1107; reputation -0.03; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1950.04** `ml18-inheritance.conditional-will::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-the-workshop-and-forfeit` tags: inheritance, legal, family, money, conditions, cinema, quiet, vienna
  Rosa把影院里的放映机、折叠椅和几卷旧片子一起卖给另一家影院，得了十二克朗；遗嘱里的六十克朗随即转给Josef 2。Helene Winter 3把文件收进皮包，说这是合法的，只是姑婆大概不会喜欢。Rosa用那笔钱换了新窗帘，旧影院的门上钉了一块出租牌。
  _effects: cash 22 (sale of the workshop equipment and the forfeited first payment); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004413:child; flag +broke_the_will_conditions; knowledge “只要影院关门，剩下的五十克朗就归那个旁支亲戚。”; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.06** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hearse-as-taxi` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Rosa拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Helene Winter 3把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Rosa没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Katharina Winter起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1106; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.12** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/tell-the-landlord` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, vienna
  Rosa把梦告诉房东。房东笑了一声，说老房子下水慢，做梦都带水汽。第二天，房东带人来查，真在墙里找到一条裂缝，顺手把地窖的租金减了一克朗。Rosa把省下的钱买了一盏新灯，放在楼梯口。
  _effects: cash 1 (房东减掉的一克朗地窖租金); reputation 0.020000000000000004; knowledge “房东因为我的梦查了墙，减了地窖租金。”; flag +df18_landlord_fixed_crack; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Rosa仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Rosa学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 25 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.009999999999999995; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Rosa是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Rosa在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 30 -> 26; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 25 -> 27; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Emil Bauer 3把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Rosa把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Emil Bauer 3低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.01999999999999999 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

- **1951.03** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Rosa转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Luise Winter还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1951.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa看Mitzi Stein 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Mitzi Stein 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Rosa门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Rosa打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Rosa去市政办事处登记申请更大的市营住宅。办事员收下表格和两克朗手续费，说前面还有几百户，至少要等两年；Rosa把回执折好，回家路上看见新盖的市营楼房，窗户亮着，门却还进不去。
  _effects: cash -2 (municipal housing application); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Rosa在楼里替教父Otto Lechner 2问了一圈，最后在洗衣妇家找到一张空床，每月三克朗。Otto Lechner 2把箱子搬过去，Rosa替他垫了第一个月的房租；两人在楼梯口分开，Otto Lechner 2说等找到工就还。床板短了一截，箱子只能竖着放。
  _effects: cash -3 (first month’s bed rent for the relative); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; role; knowledge “洗衣妇家有一张空床，每月三克朗。”_

  Anna Weiss把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Rosa解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Berta Weiss递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  Luise Moser 2把笔录推过来，说只要说出时间，不算出卖告解。Rosa写了“十一点一刻”，又把笔放下，没有写名字。Luise Moser 2盯着那半页纸看了很久，最后说：这也够一个人坐牢了。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +gave_confession_time_to_police; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; knowledge “the police can place the arson at eleven fifteen”_

  神父把死者的名字念错了一个音，Maria Lechner家的孩子先笑出声，又赶紧捂住嘴。Rosa低头看鞋尖，发现鞋上沾着墓园的泥。回去的路上，大家开始用那个错名字称呼死者，仿佛这样就能让悲伤轻一点。

- **1956.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Anna Weiss在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Rosa。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Rosa没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.05; mood -0.06000000000000005; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1301; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; flag +office_under_siege_

### 224 subject events

- **1912.10** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Katharina Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Emil抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.039999999999999994; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Emil忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josef Bauer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.02; flag +first_word_door_

- **1914.06** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Friedrich Hofer正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Emil往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Emil问是什么，Josef Bauer只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1915.09** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/imaginary-friend` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  院子里没有别的孩子，Emil给墙上的裂纹起了一个名字，每天吃饭时给它留一个位置。家里的人没有纠正，只在摆碗时多放一只空碗；Josef从门口经过，问Emil在等谁。
  _effects: skill; flag +imaginary_friend; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1917.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Emil一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Katharina Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Emil抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Emil哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Emil Weiss从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Emil。Emil把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1918.01** `kernel-child.sibling-illness::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-illness-home-nursing` tags: childhood, family, illness, care, night, rural, agriculture, small_town
  没有钱请医生。Josef Bauer把旧布煮过，敷在Elisabeth Bauer胸口，又用勺子喂米汤。Emil整夜坐在床尾替Elisabeth Bauer换布，天亮时手背烫出了红印。三天后烧退了，Elisabeth Bauer瘦得锁骨突出，Josef Bauer却开始咳嗽。谁也没有再提请医生的话。
  _effects: cash -2 (cloth, fuel and rice for a sick child); health -0.06000000000000005 (fever left the child weak); health -0.030000000000000027 (night nursing and lost sleep); condition; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1918.12** `iw18-home.first-memory::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-first-snow` tags: childhood, first_memory, fire, family, wonder, fear, rural, agriculture
  Emil清早醒来，窗台上积了一层白，玻璃上的冰花像羽毛。Josef Bauer还没生火，屋里冷得能看见呼吸；Emil把手指按在窗上，留下五个小洞，又隔着洞看楼下的送煤车。那天是Emil记得的第一个冬天。
  _effects: mood 0.04; flag +first_memory_snow_

- **1918.12** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-guest-named-by-the-child` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Emil从碗橱里多拿了一只缺口的盘子，摆在灶边那把没人坐的椅子上，还认真地告诉屋里的人：今天有客人。盘子里放着一小块土豆和一片面包边。Josef Bauer问客人叫什么，Emil说了一个从没听过的名字。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:24; food; mood 0.04; flag +invisible_guest_named; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1918.12** `iw18-friend.sugar-cube::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cube-disappears` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, food, family, comic, rural, agriculture
  Emil把家里最后一块方糖放在空椅子旁边的碟子里，说是给朋友留的。下午糖不见了，Emil一口咬定是朋友吃了；晚上Klara从口袋里摸出黏着糖渣的纸，笑得直不起腰。Emil追着Klara绕了三圈桌子。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 60 -> 64; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 25 -> 28; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000004_

  又过了一年，Emil还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Emil知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Emil把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 64 -> 67; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 28 -> 30; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Emil把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Emil踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Emil单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Elisabeth Bauer擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Emil把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Elisabeth Bauer看。

  又过了一年，Emil还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Emil知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Emil把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 67 -> 70; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 30 -> 32; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Emil拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Otto Winter弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Emil接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1921.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state

  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:24; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Friedrich Hofer家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Emil递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Friedrich Hofer端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Emil把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1922.06** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil正低头看卡上的皇帝胡子，一个穿背带裤的男孩从侧面撞过来。卡飞出去，落在马车辙里。Emil扑过去捡，手掌被碎石划开一道口子。Klara追到巷子尽头，只抓下一块袖布。回来时Emil还蹲在地上，把沾泥的卡往袖子上擦。
  _effects: mood -0.08; condition_

  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。

- **1923.10** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Emil用牙咬断，重新来。Katharina Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Emil把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +lost_apprenticeship_to_sibling; role; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Emil就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Emil还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Emil在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (考前最后一夜的煤油); mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.01** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Emil把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.03_

  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; flag +typesetting_mentor; mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  _effects: mood -0.08; reputation 0.030000000000000002; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”_

- **1926.08** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/leave-before-the-last-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在最后一支曲子前溜出礼堂，沿着教堂外墙走了一圈。窗子里传来手风琴声，Emil数到第三遍副歌才往家走；家里问舞会好不好，Emil说人太多，脚疼。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 39; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 22; mood -0.02_

- **1926.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Emil追下车，把那只手套递还给Elisabeth Bauer。Elisabeth Bauer说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Emil回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:119; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 39 -> 44; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 22 -> 30; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.03_

  Emil翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03_

  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Emil和Anton从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Maria Weiss提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_prater_stunt_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/borrowed-specimen-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Friedrich Hofer说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Emil拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯)_

- **1928.07** `r2-leisure.scullery-punishment::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::scullery-punishment-reform/reported-to-inspector` tags: domestic_service, abuse, punishment, burn, servant, reform, medical, schooling
  检查员来查佣工合同，k. u. k. Landwehr-Infanterie, Wiener Ergänzungsbezirk让Emil把手藏在围裙下面。Emil没有藏，摊开掌心，那块新疤还没有褪成白色。检查员看了一眼，又看了一眼Ferdinand Berg 2，把名字写进本子。k. u. k. Landwehr-Infanterie, Wiener Ergänzungsbezirk说这是烫衣服不小心，谁家没有这种事。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +reported_iron_burn_to_inspector; role; knowledge “the inspector recorded the burn and the household address”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Emil把门闩推回去，说雨停了再来。Rosa Bauer 2在门外站了一会儿，把一封信从门缝底下塞进来，信封上沾着煤灰。Emil没有捡，第二天早上信封被扫进炉子，纸灰从灶口飘出来。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000001; flag +refused_follower; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the soldier left a letter under the cellar door”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  Emil在衣帽间堵住Gustav Lechner，问那两张票怎么到了Lena手里。Gustav Lechner说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Gustav Lechner的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。

  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Emil在电影院后门捡到一叠传单，纸背还印着上周的片名。上面写着罢工和面包，字迹歪斜。Gustav Lechner说警察已经到街口，Emil把传单塞进煤箱，只留下一张垫桌脚。
  _effects: knowledge “传单是用电影票纸印的，印的人就在附近。”; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000099:child; flag +hid_revolution_leaflets; mood -0.030000000000000006_

- **1930.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.bad-blood` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Lena的母亲把Emil带来的点心原封退回，说家里不娶有肺病亲戚的人。点心是早上排队买的，油纸还热着，Emil把它抱在胸前。Lena站在门后没有出来，只让妹妹传话。Emil把戒指留在门房，走回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时天还没黑，楼下的狗一直叫。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +marriage_refused; cash -2 (pastries and the returned ring)_

  Emil和Franz Bauer 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Franz Bauer 2在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; mood 0.04; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:119; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.cheap-picture` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Emil和Elisabeth Huber买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Emil把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.05; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1930.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Gustav Stein拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1930.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Berta Weiss在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1930.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外是维也纳第十五区的一条窄街，卖烤栗子的炉子冒着白烟。Emil贴完纸退后两步看，发现“欠”字被浆糊洇开了一点。老成员没说话，只把眼镜摘下来擦了又擦。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-by-the-day` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Emil天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Emil把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。
  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +day_labour_only_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Emil把报纸推到一边，和Gustav Moser聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Emil起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1931.01** `major-work-dismissal/leaves-before-dismissal` tags: work, dismissal, unemployment, major
  Emil在更衣室听见自己的名字被念到“下周停工”那一栏。没等账房把信封封好，Emil先开口说家里有事，要自己走。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Emil写一张自愿离职的纸，说这样双方都体面；Maria Weiss在门口摇头。走出厂门时，Emil的工票还在口袋里，辞工证明却没有。
  _effects: unemployment (left before the dismissal took effect); cash -5 (lost final settlement); role; flag +left_before_dismissal; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1931.03** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sell-the-sighting` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, reform, medical
  Emil没有排队，转身去找街角的记者。Hermann Weiss 2在后面喊“别堵路”，Emil已经钻进电话亭。第二天，小报角落里登了一句话：某人在面包队里看见了那张脸。Emil拿到几张钞票，也记住了记者没有问消息是真是假。
  _effects: cash 2 (sold the sighting to a newspaper); state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:274; relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +sold_historical_sighting_

- **1931.11** `r2-leisure.horse-race::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/won-on-the-outside` tags: leisure, horse_racing, betting, debt, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  最后一道弯，Emil押的“黑旋风”从外道切进来，马蹄把泥甩到栏杆上。Elisabeth Bauer抓着Emil的袖子，指甲掐进布里。冲线时，Friedrich Kral 2在围栏边低下头，像是在祈祷。Emil拿着彩票去兑钱，窗口后面的人数了三遍，才把一大叠钞票推出来。
  _effects: cash 20 (最后一道弯); mood 0.12; flag +won_horse_race_bet; knowledge “Black Whirlwind runs best on the outside rail”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1932.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Stein嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Gustav Stein家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Gustav Stein那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004557_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Emil在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Emil的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Emil又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 65 -> 67_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1932.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-gives-up` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在柜台前站了很久，最后把处方留在那里，说等有货再来。回家路上经过医院，看见药房窗口也挂着“无货”；第二天，处方被雨水泡烂在口袋里。Emil把湿透的处方摊在炉边烤，字还是糊了。
  _effects: cash -1 (tram fare and a paper envelope for the prescription); health -0.040000000000000036 (another day without the prescribed medicine); condition; role; knowledge “医院药房也会挂“无货”；处方留在柜台，第二天下雨就泡烂了。”_

- **1932.02** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Friedrich Hofer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Emil听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1932.06** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/warehouse-sinecure` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, reform
  Alois Bauer把Emil的名字推荐给码头仓库，说那里正缺一个“能扛两百斤的人”。Emil去了才知道，岗位是看管一只装满旧账本的暖房，工钱三克朗，主要工作是别让老鼠咬纸。Alois Bauer听说后，在工会门口堵了三天，问这算哪门子惩罚。
  _effects: cash 3 (仓库暖房的看管工钱); role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +enemy_recommended_a_sinecure; mood 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1932.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Josef借给Emil一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Emil睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.06_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004550_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Franz Bauer 2在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Franz Bauer 2把煤钱推过来，Emil接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; mood -0.03_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Emil又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 67 -> 69_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Emil把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Katharina Bauer 2在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Emil站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1933.01** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006213_

- **1933.08** `r2-law.smuggling-cigarettes::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/porter-double-crossed` tags: crime, smuggling, customs, risk, stations, vienna, urban, crisis
  货是送到了，Mitzi Lechner却把货款卷走，只留下一张纸条，说“下回再算”。Emil去找Luise Hofer打听，Luise Hofer说Mitzi Lechner昨晚上了去边境的火车，票是提前买好的。Emil把纸条翻过来，背面还写着一行：棺材钱你还没付。
  _effects: cash -12 (棺材夹层里的卷烟); flag +smuggling_partner_absconded; relation rival with person-mitzi-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the porter bought a ticket to the border before the delivery”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1933.12** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/leave-early` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  Emil在第二本胶片放到一半时起身，摸黑从侧门出去。Josef Kral在门口抽烟，问是不是片子太旧。Emil说不是，是座位太硬；街上的雨刚停，海报被泡得只剩半张脸。
  _effects: mood -0.009999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +left_newsreel_early; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1933.12** `r3-crime.vigilante-justice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/arrested-with-the-crowd` tags: crime, vigilante, violence, village, law, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Weiss 2没有先抓Anna Moser 4，而是让所有人靠墙站好。Emil Weiss说“我们抓的是贼”，Hermann Weiss 2说“我抓的是打人的”。Emil被记下名字，罚了八克朗。走出警局时，Anna Moser 4正坐在台阶上，手里捧着一杯水，没有人看他。
  _effects: cash -8 (磨坊后面立着一根新木桩); condition; flag +arrested_after_vigilante_justice; role; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “the constable charged the crowd, not the suspect”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  Emil忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Emil听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 69 -> 61; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/no-record` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Emil Lechner把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Maria Kral不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Emil扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); mood -0.18; condition; flag +missing_relative_no_record; relation kin with person-maria-kral; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”_

- **1934.03** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-and-let-the-cousin` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, vienna, urban
  Emil在公证处坐了很久，最后说自己没有力气再经营一间铺子。Josefine Hofer接过钥匙，脸上先是惊讶，随后是一种被托付的沉重。半年后，那间印刷所改了招牌，Josefine Hofer在门口看见Emil，只点了点头，没有提请客吃饭的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (the notary's fee for refusing the inheritance); relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; flag +refused_print_shop; knowledge “叔父的印刷所欠着十四克朗，订阅名单是唯一还值钱的东西。”; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  车间里管互助会的Rosa Moser在午休时把本子摊在Emil的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Emil从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Rosa Moser当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000332:child; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  赌场经理把Emil请到柜台后面，端来一杯咖啡，说这套法子已经在别的桌上被用过了。他们不查牌，只请Emil以后别再来；作为补偿，柜台退给Emil十二克朗的车马费。Franz Bauer 2在门口笑出声，说这是Emil第一次靠赢钱被赶出去。
  _effects: cash 12 (the casino's travel compensation); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:390; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; reputation 0.010000000000000009; flag +banned_from_casino; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.03_

  账本的纸边已经发毛，Emil翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。

  Hermann Weiss 2把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Emil去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Anna Lechner 2给Emil一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Emil把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  夜里疼醒时，Emil摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Anna Lechner 2第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Emil只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.020000000000000018 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

- **1935.06** `kernel-health.mental-breakdown::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::mental-crisis-police-transport/mental-crisis-police-ward` tags: health, mental, exhaustion, asylum, crisis, vienna, urban, war
  两个巡警把Emil从电车站带走，说有人在铁轨边看见他站了半个钟头。区公所的医生只问了三个问题，就签了送精神病院的单子；警车里还有一个人一直在笑。警车经过电车站时，那个人还在笑。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; health -0.09999999999999998 (police transport and sedation); condition; role; flag +police_transport; cash -5 (transport and lost belongings); knowledge “巡警可以把在铁轨边停留的人带去区公所，医生只问三个问题就能签精神病院入院单。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1935.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil数出八赫勒交给Maria Kral，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Maria Kral说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Emil盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Emil带着Gustav Weiss去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Gustav Weiss一直捏着那张课程表，Emil则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。

  Emil又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 53 -> 55_

  Emil连续值了三个夜班，给二十七个伤员换药。最后一个伤员抓住Emil的手腕，叫了一个陌生的名字。Emil没有抽回手，直到他睡着。交班时，Emil发现自己把日期写错了，又用钢笔描了一遍。窗外的天已经亮了，走廊里还有人呻吟。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (病房里的第一针); mood -0.04999999999999999; condition_

  揭幕到一半，穿黑纱的Otto Weiss从第二排站起来，说那根火柴是她丈夫一八九三年在布尔诺登记的，专利号还刻在家里一只锡盒上。Hermann Weiss 2请她进办公室核对文件，Emil被留在展厅里，面对一队已经买票的小学生。第二天，铭牌换成了“无名热心市民”，Emil的名字被刮掉，只剩一道白痕。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:475; cash -1 (退掉车马费后的车钱); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; condition; flag +plaque_scraped_clean; mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Otto Weiss把Emil在饭桌上说过的名字和地址卖给了别人。几天后，Emil发现自己的话出现在一封不该出现的信里。Otto Weiss没有否认，只说“大家都要活”。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:474; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  隔壁把晾衣绳往Emil这边挪了一尺，水滴正好落在窗台上。Emil拿粉笔在墙上画了一道线；Luise Kral看见了，又把绳子挪回去半尺。两个人都觉得自己让了步，晚饭时却都把窗户关得很紧。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +laundry_line_dispute_

  _effects: cash -1 (帮两位老小姐买煤); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; skill; condition; flag +kept_the_recipe; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 22 -> 29; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 1; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  话在喉咙里滚了几遍，终究没有等到对方进门。门一响，有人就把紫罗兰放在门槛边，转身往街角走。花被早晨的扫帚拨到墙根，对方捡起来闻了闻，追到巷口只看见一个拐弯的背影，午后托人捎来一句话，说钱的事再算。
  _effects: cash -0.24 (一束紫罗兰); relation friend with person-marie-huber; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.02** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Emil走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000009 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1937.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Maria Kral在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Maria Kral敲开Emil的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1937.10** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Emil把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Emil的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter)_

  Emil在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Hermann Weiss 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Emil带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-marie-huber_

  Emil把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Klara的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Emil在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。

  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租)_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: food; health -0.040000000000000036 (reduced rations); condition_

- **1940.06** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
