  Anton喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Franz Berg在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.08** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::liberation-anniversary/refused-election-pressure` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, postwar
  解放后的第一场选举，Anton在投票站门口遇见Hermann Weiss 2。Hermann Weiss 2已经换上西装，问Anton投谁；Anton说“投那个没在占领期开过会的”。Hermann Weiss 2笑了笑，把一支烟递过来，Anton没接。
  _effects: flag +refused_partisan_election_pressure; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; knowledge “the partisan is backing a candidate”_

- **1945.11** `p21-returned-enemy::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/the-hand-extended` tags: enemy, return, betrayal, revenge, debt, blacklist, history, public
  Mitzi Stein在普拉特街的咖啡馆里坐到Anton对面，把一张发黄的借据推过来，却没有提还钱。他说自己接了一笔搬运生意，缺一个信得过的人；Anton看见借据背面还留着当年两个人的签名，墨迹已经发灰。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; flag +old_enemy_alliance; cash 2 (an advance on the joint commission); knowledge “借据背面还留着当年两个人的签名，墨迹已经发灰。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Josef 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 39 -> 34; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 71 -> 76; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.cheap-picture` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Anton和Franz Huber买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Anton把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1946.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Emil Winter在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Emil Winter敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001461:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anton 2这周主动擦了黑板。Anton把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 43 -> 46; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014483_

  停电后的第三个小时，Mitzi把收音机搬到楼道，电池快没电了。女播音员说“请市民保持镇静”，随后是一段军乐。Anton听见有人开始收拾行李；楼下的铁门被风撞了三次，像有人在敲。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:922; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +heard_radio_rumor_

  旧账本摊开，纸页上的铅笔字迹深浅不一。Lena念一笔，Anton就在边上记一笔，Franz Lechner 2的钢笔悬在遗嘱上方没有落。Josefine Hofer咳了一声，说屋顶那八克朗是自己看着修的。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131; flag +demands_estate_accounting_

  鹦鹉在公证处忽然开口，反复说“盒子在钟后面”。Franz Bauer 2脸色变了，Helene Winter 3说鸟只会学舌；可回家一找，钟后面真有一只铁盒，里面是姑妈早年存下的银器。亲戚们打起了官司，Anton作为养鸟人出庭，拿了三克朗证人费，也记住了Helene Winter 3在庭上擦汗的样子。
  _effects: cash 3 (出庭作证的证人费); reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; knowledge “公证人知道钟后面的铁盒，却从没在遗嘱里提起。”; condition; flag +parrot_revealed_the_silver; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

  Helene Stein 2在等待名单上写下Anton的名字，编号在四百名以后。Anton搬进一间临时隔出的房间，厨房和厕所与另一家共用，夜里能听见隔壁的收音机。两年里，Anton每月去问一次进度，Helene Stein 2每次都翻到同一页，说“今年动不了，明年再看”。
  _effects: cash -2 (废墟上的三层砖); mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +housing_waitlist; knowledge “the housing list is more than four hundred names long”_

  Anton去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Franz Lechner 2路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-131_

  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Anton把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); skill_

- **1948.03** `r3-mig.passport-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-passport-exchange/window-closed-at-noon` tags: migration, papers, queue, bureaucracy, work, vienna, urban, postwar
  天没亮Anton就到市政厅门口排队，前面已经有八十多人。铁门十一点才开，Franz Lechner 2出来挂了一块牌子：今日号已发完。队伍没有散，雨把号码纸泡烂了。Franz Lechner 2说下周四再来，Anton算了一下，那天正好轮到自己上工。
  _effects: cash -1 (雨里的护照队); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +missed_passport_window; knowledge “passport numbers are exhausted before noon”_

  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1949.01** `r14-fam.resign-and-nurse::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hire-a-day-nurse` tags: task, family, care, aging_parent, career_sacrifice, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton没有辞职，把二十克朗交给介绍所，请了一个白天来喂药、擦身的女人。Josefine Hofer嫌外人手重，第一天就打翻了两次碗。Anton下班回来，先看账本，再看母亲的背；夜里坐在厨房里，觉得比在车间还累。
  _effects: cash -20 (辞职信和药碗); mood -0.050000000000000155; condition; flag +paid_for_care; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999_

  人事科的门开了又关，Anton的档案被放在最下面。没有人解释为什么，只有Mitzi 2把一叠旧表格推过来，说“先把这个月做完”。窗外的口号声一阵高一阵低，处里的打字机一直没有停。
  _effects: state_add institution-purge state:world-vienna-1912:1053; knowledge “审查名单还没有公开，但人事科已经把所有档案重新排过。”_

  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; skill_

  _effects: cash 9 (五分利，按周算); condition; flag +took_shark_loan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; knowledge “the lender charges five percent weekly plus a collection fee”_

  Anton只把四十赫勒会费放到桌上，让Hermann Winter在册子上画了押，整晚没开口。散会后帮着把长凳叠到墙角，收起那张发言名单，Hermann Winter说下次投票别忘了来。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (交当月工会会费)_

- **1952.01** `initiative:found_process:building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536`
  环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 环城大道写字楼管理处的持续运动; historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1952 as organizer; historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1952 as organizer; historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1952 as organizer; historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1952 as organizer; historical development:initiative-building:person-marie-huber-evt:world-vienna-1912:003536-1952 as organizer; knowledge “环城大道写字楼管理处的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.01** `historical_phase`

- **1952.01** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Anton在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。环城大道写字楼管理处把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton没有立刻答话，先去敲Luise Winter的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Luise Winter提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Anton把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1952.11** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Anton挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Emil Moser敲门时，Anton从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Anton又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `p9-work-career.reference/p9-work-career.reference.delay` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, reference, certificate
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社愿意把评语写足，说“勤勉、可靠、熟悉账目”，但要求Anton留到年底，把新来的Otto Lechner带到能单独上柜台为止。证明先不写，只给了一张临时字条。Anton拿着字条去答复新东家，对方说位置可以等到圣灵降临节。他回到柜台，把学徒的账本又翻了出来。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000236:child; flag +delayed_reference_

- **1953.03** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-take-it` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Anton把少了钱的工钱袋塞进内袋，没再问。回家路上买了两块面包，比平时少一块。
  _effects: cash -1.8 (工钱被扣); food; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1953.05** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/burn-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Anton在厨房的炉子里烧掉名单。纸灰卷起来，像黑色的叶子。Anton仍然去上工，但从那以后不再在公开场合提起环城大道写字楼管理处。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.08** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.wash-day/wash-day.shirt-in-mud` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, laundry, neighbor, household
  风是从巷口那栋新楼的方向刮过来的，绳子上的被单都鼓成了帆。衬衫落地时，几个搓衣的女人都停了手看。Anton弯腰去捡，听见身后有人说风大就早点收，别等出了事再怪人。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002119:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.10** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Otto Winter 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136_

- **1954.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.first-winery` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Anton和Mitzi Stein 2在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Maria Kral 4摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Anton输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Anton靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149_

- **1955.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Anton没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019742_

- **1955.01** `p9-history-public-action.recruit-coworker/brought-two` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  活动室的钟刚敲过一点，Franz Huber 就领着人来了。Anton 从铁盒里数出三张会员卡，卡片边角裁得不齐，但名字写得清楚。门外的木牌被挪了挪，靠得更稳当。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; skill_

- **1955.09** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-count-dispute` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  星期五发工钱时，Anton数出袋里少了一天的钱。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说是上回弄坏的字模要从工钱里扣，Josef 2在旁边盯着地板没吭声。
  _effects: cash -1.8 (被扣的字模赔偿); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1955.09** `major-crisis.press-accident/hand-crushed` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health, unemployment
  送纸的滚筒没有停稳，Anton的袖口被卷进去，右手跟着进了两个铁辊之间。Franz Huber扑过去扳离合器，机器又转了一圈才停。医院截掉了食指和中指的第二关节；厂里先垫二十四克朗，六周后事故保险结算，工厂说没有适合一只手排字的位置。
  _effects: condition; unemployment (the printshop had no composing work for a one-handed worker); cash 24 (accident insurance payment assessed after six weeks); role; flag +industrial_disability; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-bauer-flat_

  嘘声从靠墙那排先起来，很快盖过 Anton 的声音。有人把帽子摔在长凳上，Katharina Winter 站起来拍手让大家安静，桌角的啤酒瓶被碰倒，淌了一小片。

  旅店老板把仓库门打开，工具箱靠在墙角，刨刃上还有木屑。老板说人走时没带，欠的房钱拿工具抵。Anton在旧货商那里把刨子和凿子卖了，换了三克朗，刚够一张回维也纳的车票。

## Hedwig

Born 1912.01, died 1916.01 (age 4).
Education: none 0; occupation: none; roles: infant, child.

### 3 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Hedwig哭了很久，Josefine Hofer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josefine Hofer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Hedwig把空碗推回Josefine Hofer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Josefine Hofer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1916.01** `statistical_death/other` tags: death
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang突发急病，高热和虚弱持续了几天。医生没能给出明确诊断，Hedwig在一个夜里去世。Anton在场。
  _effects: death (急性疾病)_
